# Ephemeral Gratitude
Categories: Great Spook Items, Uncommon items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Ephemeral Gratitude is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Ephemeral Gratitude]
Type: Quest Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EPHEMERAL_GRATITUDE
Tooltip: Traded in a blink and missed the wink, it's for those who click first and ask questions never!; * Co-op Soulbound * UNCOMMON
The Ephemeral Gratitude is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Garden Visitors
An Ephemeral Gratitude is obtained by accepting a [[The Garden|Visitor]] offer in [[The Garden]] within 2 seconds of the Visitor spawning. Once this is done, the following dialogue is displayed:
THANKFUL! You received an Ephemeral Gratitude for accepting
a commission so quickly!
## Usage
### Shop Purchase
[[Ephemeral Gratitude]] 2 Ephemeral Gratitude can be given to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] in exchange for an [[Eerie Pet]]. This can only be done once during each Alchemy experiment.
## Tips
- In order to accept visitor offers so quickly, keep materials in an [[Agronomy Sack]] and [[Agronomy Sack|Enchanted Agronomy Sack]].
- Visitors spawn between 15 and 12 minutes, depending on milestones.
  - Visitor spawning can be sped up by farming crops.
- Waiting where a visitor spawns allows for quick access.
  - If the waiting queue is full, visitors will spawn at the end of the queue.
- Clicking the visitor twice allows you to skip their dialogue.
- You can use a Bachelor's Rose on two low-rarity visitors to ensure you buy the right items.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Added Ephemeral Gratitude.|
Linked from: [[Eerie Pet]], [[Gratitude (disambiguation)]], [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]

# Expired Pumpkin
Aliases: Expired Pumpkins
Categories: Great Spook Items, Uncommon items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Expired Pumpkin is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Expired Pumpkin]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Vargul the Unearthed]]
Buy: 0.03 [[Dark Candy]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EXPIRED_PUMPKIN
Tooltip: Bring 3,000 of these to Carrolyn in Scarleton on the Crimson Isle to permanently gain +5 Health and +12 Pumpkin Fortune.; UNCOMMON
Expired Pumpkin is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be obtained during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
[[Expired Pumpkin]] 30 Expired Pumpkin can be purchased from [[Vargul the Unearthed]] in [[Unincorporated]] for 1 [[Dark Candy]] during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]].
The total cost for [[Expired Pumpkin]] 3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) Expired Pumpkin is 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Dark Candy]].
## Usage
Once obtained, the player can bring [[Expired Pumpkin]] 3,000 Expired Pumpkin to [[Carrolyn]] in [[Scarleton]] to gain a permanent +5 [[Health]] and +12 [[Crop Fortune|Pumpkin Fortune]].
## History

|Oct 15, 2026|Fixed Expired Pumpkin being missing in [[Carrolyn]]'s UI.|

## Obtaining
A Free Spider can be obtained by hitting the iron bars containing the [[Trapped Spider|Trapped Spiders]] in the [[Spider's Den]] house with a [[Scavenged Iron Hammer]], which can be obtained by combining a [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Golden Hammer]] and [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Emerald Hammer]] in an [[Anvil]] in the [[Gold Mine]].
## Usage
[[Free Spider]] 2 Free Spiders and a [[Great Spook Talisman]] can be used to buy a [[Great Spook Ring]] from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].

# Frozen Spider
Categories: Great Spook Items, Legendary items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Frozen Spider is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Frozen Spider]
Type: Quest Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: One lonely spider sitting in the pond. Then down came the hook that pulled the spider out.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FROZEN_SPIDER
Tooltip: One lonely spider sitting in the pond. Then down came the hook that pulled the spider out.; * Soulbound * LEGENDARY
The Frozen Spider is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item obtainable through [[Fishing]].
## Obtaining
### Great Spook
The Frozen Spider has a chance to be obtained by fishing in the [[Spider's Den]] whilst using an [[Icy Sinker]] and [[Spooky Bait]] while the [[Fear Mongerer]] or the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is in the [[Village]].
## Usage
### Shop Purchase
[[Frozen Spider]] 10 Frozen Spider can be given to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] in exchange for a [[Great Spook Staff]]. This can only be done once during each Alchemy experiment.
## Bugs
- The Frozen Spider may not appear in the player's inventory after being caught. This can usually be fixed by opening the [[Storage]] or switching lobbies.
## Tips
- [[Enchanting]] a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] with [[Blessing (Enchantment)|Blessing]], [[Luck of the Sea]], [[Lure]] and [[Piscary]], and using a [[Flying Fish Pet]] grants extra [[Fishing Speed]], meaning catch time is shortened significantly.
  - Other fishing pets such as the [[Ammonite Pet]] or the [[Dolphin Pet]] can also be used, however they provide less [[Fishing Speed]].
- A [[Treasure Hook]] or [[Bronze Hunter Armor|Trophy Hunter Armor]] may be useful to prevent [[Sea Creature Chance]] from impacting drop rate.
## Trivia
- The player must have talked to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] to receive the quest in order for the Frozen Spider to be able to be obtained.
- [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] did not work on it, and the Frozen Spider drop would spawn behind the player that has fished it up.
- There was no owner priority with the Frozen Spider so anyone could pick it up once it had been dropped.
- The Frozen Spider drop rate is unknown, but it is theorized to be at around 10% chance.
- Despite the [[Museum]] having been released the day after the Frozen Spider was released, and the fact that it could not be traded, auctioned or sold, it was not marked as [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] in the item description until the next Alchemy experiments, in 2022.
- When the Frozen Spider was first released, [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players used to be able to use any fishing rod to fish them up, instead of needing to use an [[Ice Rod]], due to the time-locked nature of [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 1, 2021|Added Frozen Spider.|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Changed requirement from [[Ice Rod]] to [[Icy Sinker]].|
|Oct 16, 2025|Fixed not being able to catch Frozen Spiders on Classic profiles.|
Linked from: [[Great Spook Staff]], [[Spider (disambiguation)]], [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]

[Item Metadata]
Helmet Item ID: GREAT_SPOOK_HELMET
Chestplate Item ID: GREAT_SPOOK_CHESTPLATE
Leggings Item ID: GREAT_SPOOK_LEGGINGS
Boots Item ID: GREAT_SPOOK_BOOTS
Tooltip: Fear: +5; Full Set Bonus: Spooky (0\ 4); Lets you spook other players while worn by punching them with the Great Spook Staff.; Full Set Bonus: Fearsome (0\ 4); Monsters at or below your total Fear level will run away from you.; Obtained during the Alchemist experiments!; This item can be reforged!; EPIC HELMET
Great Spook Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] set bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].
It grants the [[Fear]] stat, which makes some [[Mob|mobs]] run away from the player if they have full Great Spook Armor equipped. For those types of mobs, they will only run away if the players' total [[Fear]] is greater than the individual mob's level.
## Obtaining
### Helmet
Bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 1 [[Wet Pumpkin]], which can be obtained by the player putting an [[Decorations|Appalled Pumpkin]] (purchased from the [[Builder]]) on their head, and submerging themselves fully in water.
### Chestplate
Bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 5 [[Decayed Bat]], which can be obtained by killing [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bats]] with a [[Wither Bow]] and [[Arrows|Icy Arrows]]. The easiest way to spawn Bats is by using a [[Roofed Forest Island]], and then building a 3x3 box around the crystal on top of the island.
### Leggings
Bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 5 [[Poisoned Candy]], which can be crafted from 1 [[Simple Carrot Candy]], 4 [[Enchanted Poisonous Potato]] and 4 [[Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison]].
### Boots
Bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] for 12 [[Sweet Flesh]], which can be obtained by killing [[Zombie|Zombies]] using the [[Sweet Axe]], which can be purchased from the [[Lumber Merchant]].
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Great Spook Helmet ([[Great Spook Armor]]); [[Fear]]: +5|Great Spook Chestplate ([[Great Spook Armor]]); [[Fear]]: +5|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Great Spook Leggings ([[Great Spook Armor]]); [[Fear]]: +5|Great Spook Boots ([[Great Spook Armor]]); [[Fear]]: +5|
[Total]
[Great Spook Armor Fear: +20]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Spooky; Lets you spook other players while worn by punching them with the Great Spook Staff. Full Set Bonus: Fearsome; Monsters at or below your total Fear level will run away from you.]
## Trivia
- To obtain the said special items, a player must talk to [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] first.
- The item lore shows the edition number of the armor piece a player received, if obtained during the 2021 or 2022 [[Tyashoi Alchemist|Alchemist Experiments]].
- Any Great Spook Armor pieces a player buys from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] except for the first of each piece a year will be automatically [[Soulbound]].
- This is the first armor set to have the [[Fear]] stat.
- Not all pieces of the armor sets were released at the same time, with the boots being first released then the leggings second, the chestplate third, and the helmet fourth.
- The armor pieces can only be obtained once per profile.
  - In the past, the armor pieces could only be obtained on one profile per Minecraft account.
  - If a player obtained the set in 2021, they were also able to obtain a second set in later years
- When the full set is equipped, the armor will begin changing colors.
  - It animates through the following colors:
⁠ #9E00B2, ⁠ #9700AA, ⁠ #9000A3, ⁠ #89009B, ⁠ #830093, ⁠ #7C008B, ⁠ #750084, ⁠ #6E007C, ⁠ #670074, ⁠ #60006C, ⁠ #590065, ⁠ #52005D, ⁠ #4C0055, ⁠ #45004D, ⁠ #3E0046, ⁠ #37003E, ⁠ #300036, ⁠ #29002E, ⁠ #220027, ⁠ #1B001F, ⁠ #150017, ⁠ #0E000F, ⁠ #070008, and ⁠ #000000.
- When using the full set, potion particles corresponding to the color of the armor will appear around the player.
- The Fearsome ability only works while the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]] is active.
## Bugs
- The Fearsome ability does not work on certain mob types. The following list is not exhaustive.
  - [[Enderman]]
  - [[Magma Cube]]
  - [[Spider]]
## History

|Oct 29, 2021|Added Great Spook Leggings.|
|Oct 30, 2021|Added Great Spook Chestplate.|
|Oct 31, 2021|Added Great Spook Helmet.|
|Oct 5, 2022|Can now be bought from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] again.|
|Oct 5, 2022|Added Full Set Bonus: Fearsome[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].|

# Great Spook Staff
Categories: Great Spook Items, Legendary items, Items, Disabled Features, Confirmations needed
Great Spook Exclusive Great Spook Staff is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Great Spook Staff]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]
Stats: [[Fear]]: +10
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GREAT_SPOOK_STAFF
Tooltip: Fear: +10; Ability: Spooker LEFT CLICK Will spook other players with a scary message when you punch them while wearing full Great Spook Armor.; Cooldown: 1m; Ability: Spook RIGHT CLICK Will make you appear as a Dead Bush to other players.; Cooldown: 15s; Obtained during the Alchemist experiments!; LEGENDARY
The Great Spook Staff is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item. It is the final reward from [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]'s quest, after the [[Great Spook Armor]]. It grants +10 [[Fear]] while held.
The Spooky Full Set Bonus of the Great Spook Armor allows a player to spook other players by punching them with the Great Spook Staff.
It can be obtained by giving the Tyashoi Alchemist 10 [[Frozen Spider]].
## Obtaining
Fishing with an [[Icy Sinker]] and [[Spooky Bait]] in the [[Spider's Den]] will give a chance of fishing up a [[Frozen Spider]]. Obtaining 10 [[Frozen Spider|Frozen Spiders]] and bringing them to [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] will give the player the the Great Spook Staff.
## Usage
Item:
Great Spook Staff [[Fear]]: +10
[Effects]
[Ability: Spooker LEFT CLICK; Will spook other players with a scary message when you punch them.; Cooldown: 60s Ability: Spook RIGHT CLICK; Will make you appear as a Dead Bush to other players.; Cooldown: 15s]
### Spooker
This item or feature is currently disabled and cannot be used.
The Spooker ability can only be used if the player is wearing the full [[Great Spook Armor]]. It can be activated by punching any player, and has a cooldown of 60 seconds. It tells the player any of the following chat messages:
- DO YOU REALLY WANT TO DELETE YOUR CURRENT PROFILE?; It will delete in 10 seconds...; 5; 4; 3; 2; 1; ...; Your profile is fine! This was just an evil troll :)
- You are now op!; Only kidding! We won't give you op; )
- You had a blacklisted <item> in your inventory, we had to delete it! Sorry!; JK! Your <item> is fine. This was just a big mean troll :)
- You died and lost <half of purse> coins!; Just kidding! All your coins are fine, this was just a big mean troll :)
- ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬; You were kicked from your guild with reason "(We need more space/The Ban Hammer Has Spoken!/bruh/F/Goodbye!/Not funny enough)"; ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬; You're fine! Nothing changed with your guild status! :)
- ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬; Friend request from [PIG+++] Technoblade; [ACCEPT] - [DENY] - [IGNORE]; ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬; Sorry, its just a spook bro. :)
The spooker is also told You spooked <player> with '<spook>'!.
Before the green message, the player is told one of the following:
- Nope!/False!/Just kidding! <player> just spooked you!.
- Nope!/False!/Just kidding! <player> spooked you!.
- You just got spooked! <player> is the culprit!
New players cannot be spooked, and the spooker is told "This player is too new to be spooked!".
### Spook
The Spook ability can be used even if the player is not wearing the full [[Great Spook Armor]]. It turns the player invisible when right-clicked (including the player's armor), and replaces them with a dead bush, saying "WOAH! your staff turned you into a Dead Bush!". It has a 15 second cooldown, and makes them a dead a bush for 10 seconds.
## Trivia
- The item lore shows the edition number of the armor piece a player received, if obtained during the 2021 or 2022[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] [[Tyashoi Alchemist|Alchemist Experiments]].
- Any Great Spook Staffs a player buys from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] except for the first one each year will be automatically [[Soulbound]].
- This is the first item (other than the [[Great Spook Armor]]) to have the [[Fear]] stat.
- One of the items the staff can claim to have been blacklisted is the [[SkyBlock Menu]] item.
- Sometimes there is a discrepancy between the chat message sent from the Spooker ability and the chat message on the receiving player's end.
- This item's Spooker ability does not work in the [[Dungeon Hub]], while its Spook ability works as intended.
- A player cannot be spooked with the Spooker ability while their Spook ability is active.
- The Spook ability removes the [[Night Vision Potion|Night Vision]] effect when used.
- The Spook ability used to work in the [[Dark Auction]] and could bypass the [[Invisibility Potion|Invisibility]] effect when used. It can no longer be used in the Dark Auction, however.
## Tips
- As this item does not have a base [[Damage]] stat and cannot be reforged or enchanted, it will generally deal very low damage to mobs. Players can either grind weaker mobs such as [[Graveyard Zombie|Graveyard Zombies]] or lower-tier [[Spider|Spiders]], or make use of the One Punch perk in the [[Essence Shops|Dragon Essence Shop]] in order to easily one-tap [[Enderman|Endermen]] and [[Zealot|Zealots]] using it.
  - Contrary to its description, One Punch is actually affected by [[Crit Damage]], therefore it can be quite powerful.
## Gallery
- In-hand model.

# Great Spook Tree
Categories: Great Spook Items, Epic items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Great Spook Tree is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Great Spook Tree]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Vargul the Unearthed]]
Obtained: The Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Vargul the Unearthed]]
Buy: 170 (2 stacks plus 42) [[Dark Candy]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPOOKY_TREE
Tooltip: Place this item on your island!; Place ONE of this tree on your island, and collect its fruits... rarely.; EPIC
The Great Spook Tree is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Furniture]] item.
## Obtaining
A Great Spook Tree can be purchased from [[Vargul the Unearthed]] in [[Unincorporated]] for 170 [[Dark Candy]] during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]].
## Usage
When placed on the [[Private Island]], the Great Spook Tree has a 10% chance to generate [[Green Candy]] (85%) or [[Purple Candy]] (15%) at 12:00 AM [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]] after 30 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]] (10 Hours) since candy was last generated. Candy is guaranteed to generate after 42 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]] (14 Hours). Only a total of 5 [[Green Candy]] and [[Purple Candy]] can be held at once.
Only one Great Spook Tree can be placed at a time. It can be picked up again by Left-Clicking it.
## Game Message
|Condition|Guaranteed Message|
|-|-|
|Taking [[Green Candy]]|You got <amount> Green Candy from the Great Spook Tree!|
|Taking [[Purple Candy]]|You got <amount> Purple Candy from the Great Spook Tree!|
|No candy generated|I don't have any candy for you right now! Go away! >:(|
|Candy generated while on the island|Your Great Spook Tree has just sprouted a fruit!|
|Interacting with a Great Spook Tree while guesting|HEY! I don't belong to you >:(|

# Math Teacher
Categories: Great Spook Items, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Great Spook Exclusive Math Teacher is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Math Teacher]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: MATH_TEACHER_100
Math Teacher is mob that spawns during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook event]], after the death of a [[Primal Fear]].
## Behavior
Math Teacher will walk slowly towards the player once spawned dealing 1 [[Damage]]. They have 5 [[Health]] and like [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit. Upon reaching 1 [[Health]], a random math equation is sent in chat and the player must type the answer in the chat to be able to defeat Math Teacher.

# Obscure Ending
Categories: Great Spook Items, Rare items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Obscure Ending is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Obscure Ending]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Darkened by shadow.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OBSCURE_ENDING
Tooltip: Darkened by shadow.; * Soulbound * RARE
The Obscure Ending is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
Obscure Ending can be obtained by talking to [[Guber]] after completing the [[End Race]] with a [[Blindness Potion|Blindness I Potion]] active all the way.
## Usage
Obscure Ending can be used to buy a [[Great Spook Necklace]] from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].
## Tips
- By throwing [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] or [[Silent Pearl|Silent Pearls]] upwards at the checkpoints with precise timing, you can avoid walking.
  - You should also have unlocked the [[Travel Scroll to the End]] to be able to start the race before your pearl lands.
## History

|Oct 5, 2022|Added Obscure Ending.|

# Primal Fear
Categories: Great Spook Items, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Great Spook Exclusive Primal Fear is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Primal Fear]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 29
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5; [[Damage]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Green Candy]] 1x [[Purple Candy]] 1x [[Dark Candy]]
XP: +43 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: PRIMAL_FEAR_100
Primal Fear is a hostile [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook event]].
## Spawning
Primal Fears have a 10% chance to spawn when a mob is killed during the Great Spook every 6 minutes if the player has 0 [[Fear]].
The cooldown is reduced by 3 seconds for each point of the player's [[Fear]] stat. The timer starts once a Primal Fear is summoned. Cooldown cannot be lower than 15 seconds.
The base cooldown is always 6 minutes, and the time reduction by [[Fear]] will be subtracted from the base time. Any changes of [[Fear]] will be reflected on the cooldown instantly. Because of this, the current [[Fear]] at the moment of spawning a Primal Fear doesn't affect the cooldown of the next one.
## Behavior
Primal Fears will slowly walk toward the player once spawned, dealing 1 [[Damage]]. They have 5 [[Health]] and, like [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit. The only way to increase this is using the [[Eerie Pet]]. Upon death, they spawn a manifestation ( [[Commitment Phobia]], [[Darkness Shade]], [[Deadline]], [[Math Teacher]], [[Public Speaking Demon]] or [[Vegan Crawler]]).
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv29]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dark Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Purple Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv29]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|43|43|43|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|
## Trivia
- [[Looting]] does not work on Primal Fears or their manifestations.
- If a Primal Fear falls into the void, the player still receives the drops of both the primal fear and its manifestation.
- If a Primal Fear despawns due to an [[Ender Dragon]] dying, the player gets the rewards for killing it, but its manifestation does not spawn.
## Bugs
- Occasionally, a Primal Fear will not die after 5 attacks and requires a 6th hit to kill it.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 25, 2026|Changed the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) and non-item drops.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Changed the [[Shielded]] [[Health]] multiplier in the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) calculation from 1,000x to 100,000x, impacting this mob's level and non-item drops.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Primal Fears mentioned in Patch Notes.|
|Oct 30, 2023|Primal Fears can now be spawned.|
|Oct 31, 2023|Increased cooldown reduction of [[Fear]] to 3 seconds per point.|
|Oct 31, 2023|Primal Fears can no longer spawn in the [[Rift Dimension]].|
|Oct 31, 2023|Primal Fears can no longer be runic or corrupted.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Primal Fears now also drop candies, not just their manifestations.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed the Mob Level ([[Mob]]) from [Lv100] to [Lv29], [[Coins]] from 0 to 9, [[Experience]] from 0 to 31, and [[Combat]] XP from 6 to 43.|

# Public Speaking Demon
Categories: Great Spook Items, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Great Spook Exclusive Public Speaking Demon is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Public Speaking Demon]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 29
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5; [[Damage]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Green Candy]] 1x [[Purple Candy]] 1x [[Dark Candy]]
XP: +43 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: PUBLIC_SPEAKING_DEMON_100
Public Speaking Demon is a mob that spawns during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook event]] after the death of a [[Primal Fear]].
## Behavior
Unlike other [[Primal Fear|Primal Fears]], the Public Speaking Demon spawns in a group of 5. They will walk slowly toward the player once spawned, dealing 1 [[Damage]]. They have 5 [[Health]] and like [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit. Upon reaching 1 [[Health]], they encourage the player to speak, with which the player must comply to be able to damage the Public Speaking Demon again. The message must be at least ten characters long.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv29]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dark Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Purple Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv29]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|43|43|43|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Public Speaking Demon: Speak [name]!|
|After reaching 1 [[Health]]|[FEAR] Public Speaking Demon: Say something interesting [name]!|
|After the player says less than 10 characters|[FEAR] Public Speaking Demon: Too short! Say more!|
|After the player says 10 or more characters|[FEAR] Public Speaking Demon: Woohoo! Thank you for speaking out loud!|
## Trivia
- If any of the Public Speaking Demons fall to the void, the others despawn and the player gets rewarded as if they killed them.
- A Public Speaking Demon may display dialogue from a [[Darkness Shade]] above its head.
- When it was first released, muted players were unable to complete the Public Speaking Demon's quest. This was fixed a day later.
## Bugs
- Sometimes not all of the Public Speaking Demons lose [[Health]] upon the others being hit. This allows the player to hit one that still has more [[Health]] after one drops to 1 [[Health]], bypassing the speaking requirement.
- While in the [[Catacombs]] as a Berserk, Public Speaking Demons cannot be damaged while holding a weapon due to a bug with the Weapon Master class ability. However, they can be damaged with the player's fist.
## History

|Aug 25, 2026|Changed the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) and non-item drops.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Changed the [[Shielded]] [[Health]] multiplier in the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) calculation from 1,000x to 100,000x, impacting this mob's level and non-item drops.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Primal Fears mentioned in Patch Notes.|
|Oct 30, 2023|Public Speaking Demon can now be spawned.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed the Mob Level ([[Mob]]) from [Lv100] to [Lv29], [[Coins]] from 0 to 9, [[Experience]] from 0 to 31, and [[Combat]] XP from 6 to 43.|

# Scavenged Iron Hammer
Categories: Great Spook Items, Special items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Scavenged Iron Hammer is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Scavenged Iron Hammer]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Item ID: DWARVEN_IRON_HAMMER
Tooltip: Strong enough to break the toughest bars.; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL
The Scavenged Iron Hammer is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item.
## Obtaining
The Scavenged Iron Hammer can be obtained by combining a [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Golden Hammer]] and [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Emerald Hammer]] in an [[Anvil]] in the [[Gold Mine]]. It can only be obtained while the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
## Usage
Hitting the Iron Bars containing the [[Trapped Spider|Trapped Spiders]] inside [[Grandma's House]] in the [[Spider's Den]] with the Scavenged Iron Hammer will free the Trapped Spiders and give the player 1 [[Free Spider]]. There is a ~5 second cooldown before you can collect another [[Free Spider]].
## Trivia
- Despite having the prefix "Scavenged", the item cannot do anything in the [[Mines of Divan]].

## Obtaining
### Brewing
Recipe: [[Decoy]]/[[Inflatable Jerry]]/[[Spirit Leap]]/[[Trap]] → Secret I Potion, Secret I Potion, Secret I Potion
[[Lava Sea Creature Armor]], [[Monster Hunter Armor]], or [[Monster Raider Armor]] must be worn to brew a Secret I Potion.
## Usage
A Secret I Potion can be given to [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] in exchange for an [[Alchemist Recipe]].
## Trivia
- When trying to drink this potion, the hotbar will change left and the message You really don't want to drink this. will show up.
  - If the player fills their entire hotbar with this potion and attempts to drink it while facing a block or moving back to the hotbar slot with the potion immediately after it changes hotbar slots, the game will eventually allow them to begin the sipping animation.
  - However, they can never actually drink the bottle.

# Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars
Aliases: Shadows Indicate Riddles Illuminating Unseen Stars, Shadows indicate riddles illuminating unseen stars
Categories: Great Spook Items, Epic items, Items
Great Spook Exclusive Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars]
Type: Quest Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SIRIUS_BOOK
Tooltip: Penned by the enigmatic merchant, Sirius, this tome unveils his 'humble' origins, draped in shadow and affluence. A tale of ambition where modesty meets grandeur.; * Co-op Soulbound * EPIC
Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars is obtained by talking to [[Sirius]] with exactly 7,871,265 [[Coins]] in the player's purse. The coins will not be spent and remain in the player's purse.
## Usage
Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars can be given to [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] in exchange for a [[Scary Grimoire]].
## Tips
- To avoid accidental coin gain, an [[Emerald Ring]], [[Talisman of Coins]], and any of their upgrades should be removed from the player's inventory and [[Accessory Bag]].
## Trivia
- According to [[Sirius]], this book is about his childhood.
- Combining the first letter of each word in the name spells Sirius.
- The amount of [[Coins]] required is based on the number of characters in each word of the name.
- Ignoring upgrades that can affect some items' names, this item has the second longest name in all of SkyBlock.
  - The first being [[Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear]].

# Sleepy Hollow
Categories: Great Spook Items, Epic items, Items, Minion Modifiers
Great Spook Exclusive Sleepy Hollow is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Sleepy Hollow]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Vargul the Unearthed]]
Obtained: The Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SLEEPY_HOLLOW
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. Place it in ONE minion and it will occasionally generate Purple Candies!; EPIC
Sleepy Hollow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] item.
## Obtaining
A Sleepy Hollow can be purchased from [[Vargul the Unearthed]] in [[Unincorporated]] for 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Dark Candy]] during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]].
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Sleepy Hollow can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It is limited to one minion per profile at a time. For every item that the minion harvests, it has a 0.00015 (1 in 6666.67) chance to generate a [[Purple Candy]]. Minions that collect multiple items at once, such as [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]], can make multiple purple candies at once. Sleepy Hollow does not affect the minion's speed or other resources generated by the minion.

Vargul the Unearthed, sometimes just Vargul, is an [[NPC]] found in [[Ruins]] during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]]. The player can spend [[Dark Candy]] at their shop to obtain exclusive items.
## Interface
This menu can also be accessed with the /openvargulmenu command
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|The ground beneath you trembles, and an eerie wind howls through the air... [Vargul]: Who dares disturb my eternal slumber? The tombstone cracks and shifts! [Vargul]: You better have something to offer me. Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Future Unearth Interactions|The ground beneath you trembles, and an eerie wind howls through the air... [Vargul]: Who dares disturb my eternal slumber? The tombstone cracks and shifts! [Vargul]: You better have something to offer me. Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Future Unearth Interactions|The earth shakes with knowing fury. [Vargul]: You've become a regular visitor. The tombstone practically opens itself. [Vargul]: Ah, my most loyal servant returns. Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Future Unearth Interactions|The earth shivers in anticipation... [Vargul]: Once more you wake me. The tombstone shifts, almost eager. [Vargul]: I sense you have more offerings. Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Future Unearth Interactions|A familiar rumble echoes. [Vargul]: So, you seek my favor again? The tombstone rises quicker this time. [Vargul]: You're proving to be quite useful. Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Future Unearth Interactions|The ground almost hums with energy... [Vargul]: I grow accustomed to these interruptions. The tombstone practivally leaps from the ground. [Vargul]: You've earned a place in my dark realm. Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Future Unearth Interactions|The ground rumbles, almost lazily. [Vargul]: Ah, you again... The tombstone creaks open with little fanfare. [Vargul]: Return as often as you wish, but know that my generosity has its limits. Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Future Unearth Interactions|The ground quakes once more... [Vargul]: You dare return?! The tombstone seems to mend itself. [Vargul]: Ah, it's you again. Have you brought more Dark Candies? Vargul the Unearthed has awakened!|
|Donating 10 Dark Candies ([[Dark Candy]]) after completing all milestones; Great Spook 2023|The ground rumbles, almost lazily. [Vargul]: Ah, you again... The tombstone creaks open with little fanfare. [Vargul]: Return as often as you wish, but know that my generosity has its limits.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Vargul: Sometimes, Dark Candy is not enough to satiate a gnawing hunger. Something...more, is needed.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Vargul: Your offering is appreciated.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Vargul: The world will forget your name soon enough.|
## Trivia
- Vargul, [[Alchemist]] and [[Sherry]] are the only [[Garden Visitors]] to have neither the sprite of a player nor a Villager.

# Vegan Crawler
Categories: Great Spook Items, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Great Spook Exclusive Vegan Crawler is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is present in the [[Farm]].
[Vegan Crawler]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 29
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5; [[Damage]]: 1
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Green Candy]] 1x [[Dark Candy]] 0–1x [[Purple Candy]]
XP: +43 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 31
[Metadata]
ID: VEGAN_CRAWLER_100
A Vegan Crawler is a mob that spawns during the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]], after the death of a [[Primal Fear]].
## Behavior
A Vegan Crawler will walk slowly towards the player once spawned dealing 1 [[Damage]]. They have 5 [[Health]] and like [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit. Upon dealing damage the first time, the player transforms into a [[Pig]], [[Cow]], or [[Chicken]] and cannot damage [[Mob|Mobs]]. The Vegan Crawler will then throw a [[Carrot]], [[Hay Bale]], and [[Seeds]] onto the ground. Collecting any item dropped undoes the transformation and allows the player to damage [[Mob|Mobs]] again. If the transformed player collects the item that the passive mob is attracted to, it is instantly killed.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv29]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dark Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Green Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Purple Candy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|35%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv29]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|9|9|9|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|43|43|43|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|31|31|31|
## Bugs
- A Vegan Crawler transformation causes armor pieces to visually appear duplicated.
  - This is resolved by interacting with the armor slots in the inventory or switching lobbies.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 9, 2024|Added Vegan Crawler.|
|Aug 25, 2026|Changed the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) and non-item drops.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Changed the [[Shielded]] [[Health]] multiplier in the Mob Levels ([[Mob]]) calculation from 1,000x to 100,000x, impacting this mob's level and non-item drops.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Added Vegan Crawler.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed the Mob Level ([[Mob]]) from [Lv100] to [Lv29], [[Coins]] from 0 to 9, [[Experience]] from 0 to 31, and [[Combat]] XP from 6 to 43.|

[Wet Pumpkin]

The Wet Pumpkin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] quest item.
## Obtaining
The Wet Pumpkin can be obtained by buying an [[Decorations|Appalled Pumpkin]] from the [[Builder]], wearing it, and then submerging in water.
## Usage
[[Wet Pumpkin]] 1 Wet Pumpkin can be given to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] in exchange for the [[Great Spook Armor|Great Spook Helmet]].
## Trivia
- The player must have talked to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] to recieve the quest in order for the Wet Pumpkin to be able to be obtained.
- When an Appalled Pumpkin is turned into a Wet Pumpkin, the message "Your [[Decorations|Appalled Pumpkin]] has transformed into a Wet Pumpkin!"

# A Honk and Seek Adventure
Categories: Historical content
A Honk and Seek Adventure was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
A Honk and Seek Adventure
April Fools Event
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
A Honk and Seek Adventure was an Event active for the first week of April 2025. While active, [[Chuckleton]] appeared in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] and special items that reference other items in the game became obtainable.
## Items
Chuckleton tasked the player with obtaining various items. Nearly every item referenced another item in SkyBlock.
|Slot|Item|Referenced Item|Obtaining|Chance|Reobtainable|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Suspicious Red Gift]]|[[Suspicious Red Gift]]|[[Red Gift]]|Talking to [[Chuckleton]] for the first time||✕ No|
|[[Vitamin Life]]|[[Vitamin Life]]|[[Vitamin Death]]|Killing Healthy mobs in The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])|4%; (1 in 25)|✕ No|
|[[Warden Art]]|[[Warden Art]]|[[Warden Heart]]|Killing any tier of [[Revenant Horror]]|2%; (1 in 50)|✕ No|
|[[Snow Union I]]|[[Snow Union I]]|Snow Minion I ([[Snow Minion]])|Opening any Gift ([[Gifts]])|1%; (1 in 100)|✕ No|
|[[Red Belt]]|[[Red Belt]]|Dojo Belts ([[Dojo]])|Failing a [[Dojo]] challenge|100%; (1 in 1)|✕ No|
|[[Ragna Rock]]|[[Ragna Rock]]|[[Ragnarock]]|Contributing to killing a [[Crimson Isle]] mini-boss|4%; (1 in 25)|✕ No|
|[[Novice Skull]]|[[Novice Skull]]|[[Master Skull]]|Opening a non-Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) [[Catacombs]] chest|4%; (1 in 25)|✕ No|
|[[Noisy Pearl]]|[[Noisy Pearl]]|[[Silent Pearl]]|Killing any [[Enderman]] in [[The End]]|2%; (1 in 50)|✔ Yes|
|[[Jerry Helmet]]|[[Jerry Helmet]]|[[Aspect of the Jerry]]|Crafting; Recipe: [[Move Jerry]] ×5 → [[Jerry Helmet]]||✔ Yes|
|[[Fudge Mint Core]]|[[Fudge Mint Core]]|[[Judgement Core]]|Killing any tier of [[Voidgloom Seraph]]|10%; (1 in 10)|✔ Yes|
|[[Blazetekk™ Ham]]|[[Blazetekk™ Ham]]|[[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio]]|Killing any mob on the [[Crimson Isle]]|1%; (1 in 100)|✕ No|
|[[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]]|[[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]]||Use the [[Jerry Helmet]]'s ability by sneaking repeatedly, until the message THINK! Think Jerry, THINK! appears, then consume a [[Fudge Mint Core]] while looking at a [[Warden Art]] placed in an [[Item Frame]].||✕ No|
## Trivia
- A Honk and Seek Adventure is visible in the event list for [[Autopet]] and [[Settings|Tablist Widgets]]. Therefore, it is possible that it will return in the future.

|Apr 1, 2025|Added A Honk and Seek Adventure.|

# Ancient Pig
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, The Uprising
Ancient Pig was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Ancient Pig]
Type: [[NPC]]
The Ancient Pig was an [[NPC]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] that was summoned when all 7 [[Shiny Relic|Shiny Relics]] were found. The shrine for the Ancient Pig could be found in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] after [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] became mayor, as it was his second perk.
When a [[Shiny Relic]] was found it automatically went and hovered by the [[Blood God Shrine]] and gave a copy of the relic to the player who found it. When all seven were collected, it summoned the Ancient Pig, giving various special buffs to players.
Upon the player's first click on the Ancient Pig, it started dialogue and opened a menu granting a [[Flying Pig]].
## Found Shiny Relics
|Relic #|Found By|Date Found|
|-|-|-|
|1|carten7|April 17, 2021|
|2|niszczyciel149|April 18, 2021|
|3|The_Blood_Angel|April 21, 2021|
|4|JCRobertson|April 24, 2021|
|5|Jimmicim|April 29, 2021|
|6|lzyy_|April 30, 2021|
|7|MrLopaiz|May 7, 2021|
## Ancient Pig Effects
After the 7 [[Shiny Relic|Shiny Relics]] were found, the Ancient Pig spawned, floating over the [[Blood God Shrine]]. The Ancient Pig granted a game-wide effect every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]] to players, for two real-life days time. Effects could be repeated, indicating that the effect selection was at mostly random.
When a new effect was selected, a full-screen message appeared for all players alerting them that a new effect was being selected. This was followed by a random-wheel like selection of the next effect. Once it was selected, a chat alert was sent out, notifying all players.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|New effect selected|[PIG SHRINE] The <effect title> shrine buff is now active! <effect description>|
|Possible effects|Fairy Soul Infusion Buff stats by 50% for 60s every time a fairy soul is clicked! Thunder A storm has begun bringing along some rare empowered creatures! Double Mob Drops All drops from mobs are doubled! Double Pet Stats All stats received from pets are doubled! Increased Runic Chance Increased the chance for runic monsters to spawn by 5x! Mining Experience Increased Mining experience earned by +25%! Foraging Experience Increased Foraging experience earned by +25%! Farming Experience Increased Farming experience earned by +25%! Fishing Experience Increased Fishing experience earned by +25%! Combat Experience Increased Combat experience earned by +25%!|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Ancient Pig: The 7 Pig Relics have been found, therefore I am. [NPC] Ancient Pig: People call me the Ancient Pig, and I bring good fortune in the land. [NPC] Ancient Pig: A random effect will appear every 20 minutes for the next 2 days. [NPC] Ancient Pig: The current effect is <current effect>.|
## Trivia
- Thunder's effect wasn't very specific. It made every island rain, spawning [[Skeleton Horse|Skeleton Horses]] and [[Charged Creeper|Charged Creepers]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 16, 2021|Added the [[Blood God Shrine]].|
|May 7, 2021|The 7th [[Shiny Relic]] was found, spawning the Ancient Pig.|
|May 8, 2021|Nerfed Fairy Soul Infusion from a 100% stat increase to 50%.|

# Australian Surveillance Goon
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Confirmations needed, Aura's Mayorship
Australian Surveillance Goon was significantly altered or removed after January 7, 2026. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Australian Surveillance Goon]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]])
Coordinates: -51.5 122 75.5
The Australian Surveillance Goon is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] and [[Barry Center]]. They were initially introduced as part of 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Candypat's Public Surveillance perk and required players to check in every 30 minutes. Failing to do so granted a 10% stat debuff. The duration was later increased to 12 hours and added as part of 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] MelonKingDe's Minister perks.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Checking in|[NPC] Goon: Aura thanks you for your cooperation. Come back in 30 minutes if you do not want to face the consequences.|
|Interacting right after checking in|[NPC] Goon: You've just checked in a few seconds ago... [NPC] Goon: Now go, be a productive citizen for our great society.|
## Trivia
- Australian Surveillance Goon can appear both with and without a [[Stick]] in hand, seemingly at random.
## Gallery
- Holding a stick
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

|Dec 18, 2025|Removed Surveillance Goon.|
|Jan 3, 2026|Re-added Australian Surveillance Goon.|

|Jan 3, 2026|Surveillance debuff now only affects Combat; and Misc stats[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].|
|Jan 7, 2025|Removed Australian Surveillance Goon.|

# Blazing Fortress
Aliases: Fortress, Nether Fortress
Categories: Historical content, Locations
Blazing Fortress was significantly altered or removed after 0.13. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Blazing Fortress]
Type: [[Combat]] location
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): Travel Scroll to Blazing Fortress ([[Travel Scrolls]]); Travel Scroll to Magma Fields ([[Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull]])
Level: [[Combat]] Level V
Fairy Souls: 19
← Previous Location: [[Spider's Den]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Elle of the Nether]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): Zombie Pigman ([[Pigman]]) [[Blaze]] [[Wither Skeleton]] [[Magma Cube]] [[Magma Boss]] [[Ghast]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Rotten Flesh]] [[Gold Nugget]] [[Gold Ingot]] [[Flaming Sword]] [[Magma Cream]] [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] [[Ember Fragment]] [[Bone]] [[Coal]] [[Ghast Tear]] [[Blaze Rod]]
Resources: [[Netherrack]] [[Glowstone]] [[Nether Quartz]]
The Blazing Fortress was an island based around the Nether. It was accessed after the player has attained [[Combat]] Level V by using a launch pad found at [[Coordinates|-319, 95, -314]] in the [[Spider's Den|Spiders Den]].
However, you could still access a bit of the island by using an [[Aspect of the End]], [[Spider's Boots]], or a [[Grappling Hook]] through the void in the [[Deep Caverns|Deep Caverns,]] at [[Coordinates|-53, 223, 1]]. The area you can access is limited as you automatically warp back to the spawn of the Deep Caverns if you travel too far.
The [[Crimson Isle]] replaced the Blazing Fortress in the Nether Update. A large variety of new, stronger mobs were added, and the island was expanded. The [[Combat]] level requirement is XXII (22).
## Location
Inside the fortress itself, there were [[Nether Wart]], [[Blaze|Blazes]], and [[Wither Skeleton|Wither Skeletons]]. Step 4 of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest took place there, so [[Romero]] would be found here, where you had to bring him one [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] to do the quest. The fortress also had a large courtyard behind it, connected by a bridge.
Magma Cubes spawned on the bridge connecting the two parts. In the arena, larger, more dangerous Magma Cubes spawned. At nighttime, [[Ghast|Ghasts]] would spawn very high in the sky. Every two hours, a [[Magma Cube Boss|Magma Boss]] would spawn which has a chance to drop the [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant of the [[Magma Cube Pet]] to those who dealt the most damage to it.
Outside of the fortress, there was a cave full of [[Glowstone]], [[Netherrack]], and [[Nether Quartz]]. The entrance in front of the cave was filled with [[Pigman|Zombie Pigmen]]. The cave ended with a view of the courtyard bridge.
### Temperature
The Temperature was a mechanic in the Blazing Fortress, which could be found by pressing tab. It was used to indicate when the next [[Magma Cube Boss]] was about to spawn.
These were the temperatures that were known:
- Warm
- Tempered
- Hot
- Scolding
- Volcanic
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Blazing Fortress, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Blazing Fortress
■ Talk to Elle of the Nether.
■ Battle against nether mobs.
■ Defeat the Magma Cube Boss.
■ Collect nether wart, blaze rods, and magma cream.
## Trivia
- There was a flower pot located at [[Coordinates|-213, 76, -480]] which served as a hint to players of a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] below.
- Another flower pot next to a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] at [[Coordinates|-303, 107, -691]] (via a tunnel starting at [[Coordinates|-301, 107, -704]]).
- The Blazing Fortress was actually a Desert Biome.
- When viewed from the [[Spider's Den]], snow was on the roof of the fortress.
- If you traveled to the Blazing Fortress without a launcher, I.E. using a grappling hook or an AOTE, part of the caves where you would mine [[Nether Quartz]] would be made of [[Sponge]].
- Traveling to the Blazing Fortress shortly after it was removed resulted in the message There is a disturbance, this island has disappeared...
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Replaced the Blazing Fortress with the [[Crimson Isle]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Blazing Fortress.|
|Aug 24, 2021|Fixed the 'Collect Nether Wart and Blaze Rods' objective not completing in some cases|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Replaced the Blazing Fortress with the [[Crimson Isle]].|

# Candidate Aura
Aliases: Mayor Aura
Categories: Historical content, Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Article stubs, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election, Aura's Mayorship
Candidate Aura was significantly altered or removed after January 19, 2026. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Candidate Aura]
Also known as: Mayor Aura
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
Shop: ✕ No
Candidate Aura was a mayoral candidate that served between the Year 458 and 468 [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]]. While she was in the office, regular elections were replaced with [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]].
## Mayoral Perks
Most mayors run with up to four unique perks, but Candidate Aura has only been seen with three perks and a note: "*few extra details not worth mentioning."
Candidate Aura may run for election with up to 6 possible perks.
  [[Candidate Aura]] Aura
1. Universal Income*:
2. Increased Loot*:
3. Increased Experience*:
Actual perks hidden until Aura is elected
1. Fundraising: Taxes and Costs vastly increased across the world.
2. Minion Union: Minions work +50% slower.
3. Universal Income: Every hour, 100% of the [[Coins]] gained from Fundraising are redistributed equally.
4. Work Better: Everyone is now luckier!
5. Work Harder: Monsters deal 10% more damage and have 10% more health.
6. Work Smarter: Gain +50% Skill EXP, Dungeon EXP and Slayer EXP
### Fundraising
While Mayor Aura's Fundraising perk was active, several additional costs were incurred:
- Sending any message costs 7 [[Coins]] each time (also applies in [[Stranded]]).
- Using [[Fast Travel]] costs 2,000 [[Coins]] each time (except for the [[Hub Island|Hub]], [[Private Island]], and [[The Garden|Garden]]).
- [[Bazaar]] tax and [[Slayer]] spawn costs are doubled.
- Upgrading pets at [[Kat]] costs 50% more [[Coins]].
- Certain [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] drops have increased cost.
|Slot|Item|Original Cost|Increased Cost|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bonzo's Staff]]|[[Bonzo's Staff]]|2,100,000 [[Coins]] (Emerald) 2,250,000 [[Coins]] (Obsidian)|3,100,000 [[Coins]] (Emerald) 3,250,000 [[Coins]] (Obsidian)|
|[[Dark Claymore]]|[[Dark Claymore]]|150,000,000 [[Coins]]|200,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Implosion ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]])|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|80,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Necron's Handle]]|[[Necron's Handle]]|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|265,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|5,500,000 [[Coins]] (Floor I–II) 6,000,000 [[Coins]] (Floor III+)|5,750,000 [[Coins]] (Floor I–II) 6,500,000 [[Coins]] (Floor III+)|
|[[Shadow Fury]]|[[Shadow Fury]]|15,000,000 [[Coins]]|20,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Shadow Warp ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]])|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|80,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Wither Shield ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]])|50,000,000 [[Coins]]|80,000,000 [[Coins]]|
### Minion Union
This perk actually reduced minion speed by 33% (Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.)).
This perk was disabled by minister 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid.
### Universal Income
This perk states that 100% of coins gathered via Fundraising are redistributed equally when in reality it is dictated by this formula:
Universal Income=⌊Fundraising CoinsUnique Logins at Current Hour⌋×(Seconds of Playtime at Current Hour3600 seconds)
### Work Better
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: incomplete list of items with boosted chances
See more stubs.
This perk granted +50 [[Magic Find]] and +25 [[Tracking]] globally. All [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]] progress was doubled, and the base drop rate of several items from the [[Crystal Nucleus]] and [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] were increased. Items affected included:
- [[Necron's Handle]]
- [[Divan's Alloy]]
- [[Quick Claw]]
### Work Harder
This perk increased the [[Health]] and [[Damage]] of mobs by 10%.
This perk was re-enabled by minister 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] Aura_Stay after being disabled by minister 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Confused_Goon.
### Work Smarter
This perk increased all Skill, [[Dungeons|Dungeon]], and [[Slayer]] XP by +50%.
This perk was re-enabled by minister 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] ALAND_ after being disabled by minister 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] SnowflakeKiwi.
## Ministers
Each year, Candidate Aura held a minister election from Late Spring 30th to Winter 7th, where the SkyBlock community elected their fellow players. Those with the top 7 votes were elected as ministers and got to choose their perks. Ministers who place on the podium, as well as ministers who attempt to remove Aura's perks, cannot be voted for in subsequent elections.
For perk rotations from ministers who chose the Perkpocalypse perk, see [[Candidate Aura/Perkpocalypse]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: It's time we put the people first. [NPC] Aura: No more false promises. No more slacking. No more monarchs...|
|Condition|Dialogue (in order)|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: I'm not asking for your loyalty, I'm offering you a future.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: Every coin you earn, every drop you sweat, will help everyone.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: The world doesn't need heroes. It needs workers.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: Under Dante, you worked for him. Under me, you'll work for each other.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: Let's make SkyBlock fair again.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: Dante wanted order. I want balance.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: Why should a few have it all when we can share the load?|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: Dante made you obey. I'll make you thrive.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aura: No more greed. No more chaos. Only fair progress for all.|
|Attempting to vote for an Admin|[NPC] Mayor Aura: I do not trust this person. You will not vote for them.|
|Attempting to vote for yourself|[NPC] Mayor Aura: Voting for yourself... [NPC] Mayor Aura: Where greed and narcissism tread, reprimand follows. [NPC] Mayor Aura: This will not go unseen.|
|Attempting to vote for 1dante, 1Aura, 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) Minikloon, 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) hannibal2, or 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) duoplurk|[NPC] Mayor Aura: Please select someone else.|
|Attempting to vote for a player that doesn't have any SkyBlock profile|[NPC] Mayor Aura: This citizen is not in my registry.|
|Attempting to vote for 8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]]) Technoblade|[NPC] Mayor Aura: He walked farther than any of us. Let his path remain untouched.|
|Attempting to vote for 8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]]) Technoblade|[NPC] Mayor Aura: His crown rests in a place no vote can touch.|
|Attempting to vote for 8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]]) Technoblade|[NPC] Mayor Aura: His journey continues elsewhere. We cannot call him back.|
|Attempting to vote for 8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]]) Technoblade|[NPC] Mayor Aura: His legacy votes for itself.|
|Attempting to vote for 8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]]) Technoblade|[NPC] Mayor Aura: Some names belong to the stars, not the ballots.|
|Attempting to vote for 8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]]) Technoblade|[NPC] Mayor Aura: That warrior's throne is eternal. We cannot elect eternity.|
|Attempting to vote for 8[PIG+++] ([[Ranks]]) Technoblade|[NPC] Mayor Aura: This hero has already ascended beyond out reach.|
|Attempting to vote for 7[YOUTUBE] ([[Ranks]]) im_a_squid_kid, 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) Confused_Goon, or 6[MVP++] ([[Ranks]]) SnowflakeKiwi|[NPC] Mayor Aura: They were a traitor to our great society. You will not vote for them.|
|Attempting to vote for an already elected player (unless the candidate has Long Term Investment)|[NPC] Mayor Aura: This player is currently elected. Give someone else a chance.|
|Attempting to vote for a player who has previously placed on the podium|[NPC] Mayor Aura: This person has already had their voice heard. [NPC] Mayor Aura: Perhaps give those with fewer influence a chance.|
|Sending messages critical of Aura such as: "Aura sucks" or "Aura bad"|[NPC] Mayor Aura: Don't think you're out of my grasp.|
|Issuing /killaura|[NPC] Mayor Aura: You fell for my trap, traitor.|
|Attempting to [[Fast Travel]] with under 2,000 [[Coins]] in [[Purse]] while the Fundraising Perk is active|[NPC] Mayor Aura: Progress comes at a cost. Free transport is gone for the sake of the people...|
|Attempting to send a message in chat with under 7 [[Coins]] in [[Purse]] while the Fundraising Perk is active|The cost of progress, though brazen, is unruly. Sending messages costs 7 Coins. Feed progress...|
|After the final [[Minister Election]]|[NPC] Mayor Aura: I hear the votes. [NPC] Mayor Aura: If my presence has become something to end... [NPC] Mayor Aura: Then staying would betray my own principles. [NPC] Mayor Aura: Power only works when it is wanted. [NPC] Mayor Aura: And I will not force myself into permanence. [NPC] Mayor Aura: This was the last Minister Election. [NPC] Mayor Aura: You will now choose without me. [NPC] Mayor Aura: Not because I was overthrown... [NPC] Mayor Aura: But because I am no longer welcome. [NPC] Mayor Aura: This is not defeat. It is restraint. [NPC] Mayor Aura: I will finish what I began elsewhere. [NPC] Mayor Aura: And when I return... it will be with something better.|
## Trivia
- Candidate Aura's actual perks were revealed through the SkyBlock API prior to them being elected.
  - Tablist also shows Aura as having 6 perks.
- The dialogue starts from a random point, but is otherwise always in the same order.
- Candidate Aura has broken the record for most votes received by a candidate in SkyBlock's history, reaching over 1.5m votes on November 28th, 2025.
- Upon initial release, the Work Smarter perk was also granting +50 [[Magic Find]] in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
- Some candidates were removed after gaining an unfair advantage.
  - 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] hannibal2 is the creator of the SkyHanni mod. He was blacklisted from the elections due to hannibal2's name being automatically filled in on behalf of the user, assuming they had not edited their vote.
  - 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] duoplurk was blacklisted after a [[Coins]] giveaway was hosted to bribe votes.
- Using /killaura command when Aura isn't mayor returns Aura is not mayor..
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 24, 2025|Added Candidate Aura in the year 458 [[Mayor Election]].|

# Chuckleton
Aliases: A Honk And Seek Adventure
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters
Chuckleton was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Chuckleton]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Appears during certain [[April Fools]] events.
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 3.5 78 4.5
Chuckleton is an [[NPC]] that appeared twice in the [[Village]]. He first hosted [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]] in 2025 and then the second [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]] in 2026.
## Purpose
### [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]]
During the second [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]] in April 2026, Chuckleton served as a replacement for [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]], allowing players to participate in the event, view their [[Bingo (disambiguation)|Bingo Card]], and access the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]]. He also gave items for completing goals.
### [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]]
During [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]], Chuckleton tasked the player with obtaining various items. Nearly every item referenced another item in SkyBlock.
|Slot|Item|Referenced Item|Obtaining|Chance|Reobtainable|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Suspicious Red Gift]]|[[Suspicious Red Gift]]|[[Red Gift]]|Talking to [[Chuckleton]] Chuckleton for the first time|100%; (1 in 1)|✕ No|
|[[Vitamin Life]]|[[Vitamin Life]]|[[Vitamin Death]]|Killing Healthy mobs in The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])|4%; (1 in 25)|✕ No|
|[[Warden Art]]|[[Warden Art]]|[[Warden Heart]]|Killing any tier of [[Revenant Horror]]|2%; (1 in 50)|✕ No|
|[[Snow Union I]]|[[Snow Union I]]|Snow Minion I ([[Snow Minion]])|Opening any Gift ([[Gifts]])|1%; (1 in 100)|✕ No|
|[[Red Belt]]|[[Red Belt]]|Dojo Belts ([[Dojo]])|Failing a [[Dojo]] challenge|100%; (1 in 1)|✕ No|
|[[Ragna Rock]]|[[Ragna Rock]]|[[Ragnarock]]|Contributing to killing a [[Crimson Isle]] mini-boss|4%; (1 in 25)|✕ No|
|[[Novice Skull]]|[[Novice Skull]]|[[Master Skull]]|Opening a non-Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) [[Catacombs]] chest|4%; (1 in 25)|✕ No|
|[[Noisy Pearl]]|[[Noisy Pearl]]|[[Silent Pearl]]|Killing any [[Enderman]] in [[The End]]|2%; (1 in 50)|✔ Yes|
|[[Jerry Helmet]]|[[Jerry Helmet]]|[[Aspect of the Jerry]]|Crafting; Recipe: [[Move Jerry]] ×5 → [[Jerry Helmet]]|100%; (1 in 1)|✔ Yes|
|[[Fudge Mint Core]]|[[Fudge Mint Core]]|[[Judgement Core]]|Killing any tier of [[Voidgloom Seraph]]|10%; (1 in 10)|✔ Yes|
|[[Blazetekk™ Ham]]|[[Blazetekk™ Ham]]|[[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio]]|Killing any mob on the [[Crimson Isle]]|1%; (1 in 100)|✕ No|
|[[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]]|[[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]]||Use the [[Jerry Helmet]]'s ability by sneaking repeatedly, until the message THINK! Think Jerry, THINK! appears, then consume a [[Fudge Mint Core]] while looking at a [[Warden Art]] placed in an [[Item Frame]].|100%; (1 in 1)|✕ No|

# Confused Goon (player)
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Aura's Mayorship
Confused Goon (player) was significantly altered or removed after December 12, 2025. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Confused_Goon]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Prison of the Bereft]]
Confused_Goon was an [[NPC]] in the [[Prison of the Bereft]] who was elected as a [[Minister Election|Minister]] to [[Candidate Aura|Aura]]. When he won, he attempted to remove the Work Harder perk and was sent to the [[Prison of the Bereft]].
While imprisoned, he walked around the cage and sent messages in chat. The player could vote on his freedom by talking to the [[Judicial Goon]]. Voting against freeing the prisoners granted +5 [[Magic Find]] for 2hr, and cast votes could be recast upon the effect expiring.

# Cookie Clicker
Categories: Historical content, Pages with UIs
Cookie Clicker was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Cookie Clicker]
Cookie Clicker was a minigame where players repeatedly click a Cookie.
## Mechanics
The game could be accessed from the [[Booster Cookie/UI|Booster Cookie interface]]. Starting the game would open an interface with a cookie in the middle. Clicking the cookie would add 1 to the player's cookie count. Occasionally, an enchanted cookie would appear that gave a random number of cookies. A leaderboard could be viewed at the [[Milk Bucket]]. Viewing the leaderboard required 1,000 cookies.
### Lore
After reaching a certain number of cookies, the lore in the cookie would change.
Cookie Lore:
|Cookies Required|Lore|
|-|-|
|0|You feel like making cookies. But nobody wants to eat your cookies.|
|5|Your first batch goes to the trash. The local rats barely want to touch them.|
|50|Your family reluctantly tries some of your cookies.|
|100|Your cookies are popular in the neighborhood.|
|500|People are starting to talk about your cookies.|
|1,000|Your cookies are talked about for miles around.|
|5,000|Your cookies are renowned in the whole town!|
|10,000|Your cookies bring all the boys to the yard.|
|25,000|Your cookies bring all the npcs to the hub|
|50,000|Your cookies now have their own website!|
|100,000|Your cookies are worth a lot of money.|
|1,000,000|People come from very far away to get a taste of your cookies.|
|#1|You have clicked the most cookies out of anyone, I hope you had some milk to wash them down.|
## Trivia
- In version v0.01, cookies would reset when disconnecting or swapping servers. This was changed with the addition of v0.02.
- The cookie buff is not required to play cookie clicker.
- Cookie Clicker is based on an earlier game of the same name.
- The lore for first place was discovered by 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] GetGuud, when they achieved #1 on the Cookie Clicker ranking.
- After the release of [[Chocolate Factory]] all the Cookies players had obtained were transferred into [[Chocolate]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 1, 2022|Added Cookie Clicker v0.01.|
|Feb 2, 2023|0.17.3|Added Cookie Clicker v0.02.|
|Feb 2, 2023|0.17.3|Cookies no longer reset when changing servers.|
|Feb 2, 2023|0.17.3|Added Cookie Ranking.|
|Apr 1, 2023|Added Cookie Clicker v0.03.|
|Apr 1, 2023|Added occasional bonus cookies.|
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Removed Cookie Clicker|
Linked from: [[April Fools/2023]], [[Chocolate]], [[Chocolate Factory]], [[Cookie (disambiguation)]]

The Crypt Witherlord Sword was a planned [[Rarity|EPIC]] Dungeon [[Swords|Sword]]. The sword was not obtainable, but it was listed in the [[Essence Cost|Essence Guide]]. It could be upgraded using [[Wither Essence]] and appeared to be an upgraded form of the [[Dreadlord Sword]], although the ability worked differently as the [[Dreadlord Sword]]'s ability at one point had a cooldown.
Its ability is identical to the Mega Walls Dreadlord class skill.
## Usage
Sword:

# Durability
Categories: Historical content, Article stubs, Mechanics
Durability was significantly altered or removed after September 12th, 2023. This article is retained for historical purposes.
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Durability was an item statistic seen on armor and weapons that depletes the ability to use an item. If the Durability reached 0 on an item, the item stats or ability will not function until repaired at the [[Malik]] NPC for Coins. This functionality is similar to the still existing [[Machine Fuel|Drill Fuel]] for [[Drills]].
Durability, was visible when hovering over the item in the inventory.
## Notes
- All items with Durability were mob drops from enemies in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], or purchased from [[Ophelia]].
- Durability was also displayed with the Vanilla durability mechanic, with the bar at the bottom of the item.
## Gallery
Perfect Durability Example
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Durability.|
|Sep 12, 2023|0.19.4|Removed Durability as a mechanic.|

# Elle of the Nether
Aliases: Elle Of The Nether
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Quest giver, Blazing Fortress, Quests
Elle of the Nether was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Elle of the Nether]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Warriors quest ([[Quests]])
Location: [[Blazing Fortress]]
Coordinates: -311 96 -405
Elle of The Nether was an [[NPC]] in the [[Blazing Fortress]] at [[Coordinates|-311, 96, -405]]. She was a part of the Warriors quest [[Quests|Quest]].
## Quest
Elle's quest required the player to gather 10 [[Nether Wart]] and 10 [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rods]]. Afterwards, the player needs to collect 1 [[Magma Cream]], 1 [[Glowstone]] and 1 [[Nether Quartz]]. Elle's quest had a bug, which required the player to manually collect the items without [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Intro|[NPC] Elle of the Nether: I am a conqueror. I've defeated everything from the Nether to the End and back. [NPC] Elle of the Nether: There's something about you I don't like. Perhaps it is your lack of fear for the great beast that is the Nether. [NPC] Elle of the Nether: Go forth and conquer, [Player's Name]. And don't come crying to me when you fail.|
|Not enough [[Nether Wart]] and Blaze Rods ([[Blaze Rod]])|[NPC] Elle of the Nether: I told you not to come crying to me. [NPC] Elle of the Nether: Collect nether wart and defeat blaze to collect blaze rods!|
|Having not collected [[Nether Wart]] or Blaze Rods ([[Blaze Rod]]) yet`|[NPC] Elle of the Nether: Collect glowstone, nether quartz, and magma cream to become a true master of the Nether.|
|After Quest|[NPC] Elle of the Nether: I have defeated the Magma Boss more times than I can count!|
|After Quest|[NPC] Elle of the Nether: Many of the creatures on this island drop useful brewing ingredients. Use them in a Brewing Stand to brew strong potions that will aid to your journey.|
|After Quest|[NPC] Elle of the Nether: I don't know what would I do without my Fire Resistance Potions. Probably burn.|
|After Quest|[NPC] Elle of the Nether: Use them in a Brewing Stand to brew strong potions that will aid in your journey.|

# Enchantment Table/Levels (Pre-0.10)
Aliases: Enchantment Table Levels, Enchantment Table levels
Categories: Historical content, Enchants, Mechanics
Enchantment Table/Levels (Pre-0.10) was significantly altered or removed after 0.10. This article is retained for historical purposes.
The cheapest way to get most enchantments to level V (or their highest outside of special books) is to use an [[Enchantment Table]]. However, the key when doing so is to not waste XP by using an enchantment table that takes 60 levels when one could get it with a table at level 24. This is done by adding/removing [[Bookshelf|bookshelves]] (use an item with silk touch to avoid destroying them).
This is a condensed table of the enchantment tables that shows enchantments by type, sorted by minimum level. The enchantments show only the max level to facilitate adding enchantments at an Enchantment Table.
If a high level book exists, it's possible to get a higher level, but not inside the Enchantment table (see Special Enchanted Books section).

# Event Stand
Categories: Historical content, Locations, Events, Spooky Festival, Season of Jerry, New Year Celebration, Traveling Zoo
Event Stand was significantly altered or removed after 0.24.2. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Event Stand]
Type: Event location
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -3 70 -43
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Baker]] [[Oringo]] [[Jerry]] [[Fear Mongerer]]
The Event Stand was located in the [[Village]] next to the [[Bank]] towards the South-East of the portal. It was used during SkyBlock [[Events]]. During an Event, an [[NPC]] related to the current event appeared inside the stand.
## Event NPCs
- [[Spooky Festival]] - [[Fear Mongerer]]
- [[Jerry's Workshop]] - [[Jerry]]
- [[New Year Celebration]] - [[Baker]]
- [[Traveling Zoo]] - [[Oringo]]
## Appearances
- Traveling Zoo: The Event Stand becomes a zoo with a yard on the right side of the building.
- Spooky Festival: The Event Stand becomes a giant pumpkin where the Fear Mongerer is in the center.
- Jerry's Workshop and New Year Celebration: The Event Stand becomes a giant Christmas tree resembling that of the Jerry's Workshop.
- When [[Dante]] got elected the stand got boarded up and gray which shows that Dante has canceled events.
## Trivia
- There was a glitch when events were first introduced where the Event Stand would spawn on a player's private island if the player was on their island during the second traveling zoo event. The items that spawned on the stand could be collected which would give the player 2 null banners and 5 null heads.
## Gallery
- The event stand during the Traveling Zoo The event stand during the [[Traveling Zoo]]
- The event stand during Season of Jerry The event stand during [[Season of Jerry]]
- The event stand during the Spooky Festival The event stand during the [[Spooky Festival]]
- The Event Stand after Dante got elected
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Event Stand.|
|Aug 13, 2020|0.8.1|The Event Stand was moved from 35, 71, -43 ([[Coordinates]]) (center) to -3, 70, -43 ([[Coordinates]]) (center).|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Removed Event Stand after the Hub ([[Hub Island]]) revamp.|

# Evil Jerry
Categories: Historical content, Jerry
Evil Jerry was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
Evil Jerry is a mysterious character within Hypixel SkyBlock. They represents the counterpart of the joke villager [[Jerry]]. Little is known about them.
They first appeared unexpectedly after the notice of a "bug/glitch" with the [[Unstable Dragon Armor]] smiting other players with unstable lightning strikes on public islands and one-shot killing them, with players losing their coins. This was either a real bug/glitch that occurred or the developers tried to troll the players.
Their appearance is likely an easter egg within the Hypixel community with thoughts and ideas of an evil version of Jerry who is the ruler or trying to take over SkyBlock.
### Quotes:
- One could say this game is... unstable!!
## References
Capture 1 Capture 2 Capture 3
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 19, 2020|Added Evil Jerry.|
|Aug 19, 2020|Removed Evil Jerry.|
