# Floating Islands
Categories: Historical content, Locations, Wood
Floating Islands was significantly altered or removed after V0.7.4. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Floating Islands]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Foraging I (1) ([[Foraging]]) (Higher levels are needed to access some sections)
← Previous Location: [[Forest]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Charlie]]
[Resources]
Resources: Wood ([[Wood (disambiguation)]])
The Floating Islands were a series of islands accessed via the [[Forest]] where you could gather multiple types of wood: [[Birch Log|Birch]], [[Spruce Log|Spruce]], [[Jungle Log|Jungle]], [[Acacia Log|Acacia]], and [[Dark Oak Log|Dark Oak]]. [[Leaves]] and [[Saplings]] for these trees are also obtainable here.
There were five islands total: [[Birch Park]], the [[Spruce Woods]], [[Jungle Island]], the [[Savanna Woodland]], and the [[Dark Thicket]]. They followed an ascending and clockwise pattern as the player traveled through them. They each gave a different type of wood. Various types of flowers could also be harvested there.
Since update 0.7.4, the Floating Islands were replaced by [[The Park]].
## Sublocations
|Location|Coordinates|Requirement|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Birch Park]]|-243, 79, -16 ([[Coordinates]])|Foraging I (1) ([[Foraging]])|First island of the Floating Islands. A place to get Birch Wood ([[Birch Log]]).|
|[[Spruce Woods]]|-311, 97, 9 ([[Coordinates]])|Foraging II (2) ([[Foraging]])|Second island of the Floating Islands. A place to get Spruce Wood ([[Spruce Log]]).|
|[[Jungle Island]]|-361, 114, -31 ([[Coordinates]])|Foraging III (3) ([[Foraging]])|Third island of the Floating Islands. A place to get Jungle Wood ([[Jungle Log]]).|
|[[Savanna Woodland]]|-343, 132, -82 ([[Coordinates]])|Foraging IV (4) ([[Foraging]])|Fourth island of the Floating Islands. A place to get Acacia Wood ([[Acacia Log]]).|
|[[Dark Thicket]]|-285, 143, -87 ([[Coordinates]])|Foraging V (5) ([[Foraging]])|Fifth island of the Floating Islands. A place to get Dark Oak Wood ([[Dark Oak Log]]).|
## Birch Park
Birch Park
The Birch Park is where the player enters the Floating Islands from the Forest. It can also be accessed directly from the [[Private Island|Player's Island]] by using a [[Portals|Portal]] unlocked at [[Birch Log|Birch Wood III (3)]]. A launch pad leads back to the [[Hub Island]], and another leads into the Spruce Woods.
[[Birch Log|Birch Wood]] can be collected by mining Birch Trees, which is required to beat a quest.
## Spruce Woods
Spruce Woods
The Spruce Woods can only be accessed after obtaining [[Foraging]] II, by either using the portal unlocked at [[Spruce Log|Spruce Wood III (3)]] or using the launch pad in Birch Park. A launch pad leading to Jungle Island is located here.
[[Spruce Log|Spruce Wood]] is the [[Collections|collection]] item obtainable here by mining spruce trees.
## Jungle Island
Jungle Island
Jungle Island can only be accessed after obtaining Foraging III, by either using the portal unlocked at [[Jungle Log|Jungle Wood III (3)]] or using the launch pad in Spruce Woods. A launch pad leading to Savanna Woodland is located here.
[[Jungle Log|Jungle Wood]] is the main collection item obtainable here, simply by mining them. Rarely, [[Cocoa Beans]] can be obtained by breaking a wood block that they are attached to, making it possible to obtain them before achieving [[Farming]] V.
## Savanna Woodland
Savanna Woodland
Savanna Woodland can only be accessed after obtaining Foraging IV, by either using the portal unlocked at [[Acacia Log|Acacia Wood III (3)]] or using the launch pad in Jungle Island. A launch pad leading to Dark Thicket is located here.
[[Acacia Log|Acacia Wood]] is the only collectible here, from mining the trees found here.
## Dark Thicket
Dark Thicket
Dark Thicket can only be accessed after obtaining Foraging V, by either using the portal unlocked at [[Dark Oak Log|Dark Oak Wood III (3)]] or using the launch pad in Savanna Woodland. A launch pad leading back to Birch Park is located here, but Birch Park doesn't contain a launch pad to here.
[[Dark Oak Log|Dark Oak Wood]] is the main collection item here, simply by mining them. A stack of Dark Oak Wood is required for beating Charlie's quest. The [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]] can be obtained by breaking them, making them possible to obtain before Farming V.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Floating Islands.|
|Nov 11, 2019|0.7.4|Floating Islands was replaced by [[The Park]].|
Linked from: [[Birch Park]], [[Dark Thicket]], [[Fairy Souls]], [[Island (disambiguation)]], [[Jungle Island]], [[Locations]], [[Map (Hub)]], [[Savanna Woodland]], [[Spruce Woods]], [[The Park]]

# Frost Walker

[Frost Walker]

Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])

Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: II
Applied To: [[Boots]]
[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_FROST_WALKER
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Frost Walker I Ice blocks will be created below you when you walk above water in a radius of 1 blocks.; Applicable on: Boots Apply Cost: 15 Exp Levels; COMMON
Frost Walker was an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that created [[Ice]] blocks below player on water in certain radius. It could be applied to [[Boots]].
## Obtaining
The Frost Walker [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] could be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Frost Walker I–II|[[Enchantment Table]]|
## Ability
[[Ice]] blocks below player on water in a radius of 1-2 blocks.
|Tier|Radius|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|1|
|Ⅱ|2|
## Applying
The Frost Walker [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] could be applied to [[Boots]]. Unlocking [[Ice|Ice II (2)]] granted a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Frost Walker.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|15 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|30 [[Experience]]|

# Gone with the Wind
Aliases: Gone With the Wind, Gone with The Wind
Categories: Historical content, Dwarven Mines, Events
Gone with the Wind was significantly altered or removed after 0.27.2. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Gone with the Wind]
Gone with the Wind grants Mining Speed if facing the correct direction.
Gone with the Wind was a passive event that could happen in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Mechanics
The Gone with the Wind event lasted for 15 minutes. Looking in the right direction of the 'wind' rewards the player with a [[Mining Speed]] bonus.
Bonus:
|Icon|[[Mining Speed]]|
|-|-|
|≈|0 [[Mining Speed]]|
|≈|300 [[Mining Speed]]|
|≈|600 [[Mining Speed]]|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event announced [[Crystal Hollows]]|⚑ The Gone with the Wind event starts in 20 seconds! This is a passive event! It's happening everywhere in the Crystal Hollows!|
|Event announced [[Dwarven Mines]]|⚑ The Gone with the Wind event starts in 20 seconds! This is a passive event! It's happening everywhere in the Dwarven Mines!|
|Event starting|----------------------------------------------------- GONE WITH THE WIND STARTED! Passive Active Event The cavern is windy today! Mining in the direction of the wind will give +600⸕ Mining Speed. -----------------------------------------------------|
|Event ending|----------------------------------------------------- GONE WITH THE WIND ENDED! Passive Active Event The wind has died down leading to normal mining speeds. -----------------------------------------------------|
## Trivia
- The name of this event is a reference to the popular novel Gone With The Wind by Margaret Mitchell.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Gone with the Wind.|
|Aug 25, 2026|Removed Gone with the Wind.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Gone with the Wind.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Removed Gone with the Wind.|
Linked from: [[Old Man Garry]]

# Goons
Aliases: Dante's Goons, Dante's goons, Goon
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, The Uprising, Aura's Mayorship
Goons was significantly altered or removed after January 19, 2026. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Goon]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Precise Location: Various locations across the Hub ([[Hub Island]])
Goons are [[NPC|NPCs]] present in the Hub while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] or [[Dante]] is in office.
Punching a Goon while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] is [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] sends the player to the [[Prison of the Bereft]].
Preceding [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]], they were meant to protect [[Official Buildings]] and only let the player pass if they were wearing a [[Happy Mask]] or had a [[Dante's Talisman]] in their inventory. If the player had less than 8 hours of playtime, Goons would not prevent them from entering [[Official Buildings]].
Other Goon variants, such as the [[Corrupt Goon]], [[Guard Goon]] and [[Electoral Goon]], do not jail the player when attacked.
## Dialogue
### During Aura's term
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|

## Trivia
- If a player jumped into the areas with the [[Auction Agent|Auction Agents]] or the [[Auction Master]], the Goons would still tell them to get a [[Happy Mask]], even if they had one.
  - This is possibly due to these two actions using the same pushing away mechanism.
- When clicked, Goons made a sound of a Slime jumping. It used to be a Villager mumbling with a tweaked pitch.
  - Their Slime sounds were most likely a reference to how [[Dante]] and the Goons transformed into Slimes during the [[Resistance Movement|boss fight]] against Dante.
- Goons were also near various locations like the [[Golden Dante Statue]], [[Fisherman's Hut]], and most shops, but would let the player pass without a [[Happy Mask]] or a [[Dante's Talisman]].
## Gallery
- Goons standing in front of Community Center Goons standing in front of [[Community Center]]

Hugh Pixel was an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]]. They gave out the [[Dreamspire Torch]], a [[Mementos|Memento]] for Hypixel's twelfth anniversary that had no requirements to claim. ANNIVERSARY CINEMATIC was displayed below the Hugh Pixel name tag.

# Im a squid kid
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed
im_a_squid_kid was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[im_a_squid_kid]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Prison of the Bereft]]
im_a_squid_kid is an [[NPC]] in the [[Community Center]], formerly in the [[Prison of the Bereft]].
im_a_squid_kid was elected as a [[Minister Election|Minister]] to [[Candidate Aura|Aura]]. When he won, he attempted to remove the Minion Union perk and was sent to the [[Prison of the Bereft]]. While imprisoned, he walked around the cage and sent messages in chat.
The player could use a [[Rusty Spoon]] to break the cage and make progress towards freeing im_a_squid_kid.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: hey can you guys get me out of here ples? thanks|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: absolute cinema|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: @hypixel pls get me out|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: SOMEBODY DO SOMETHING!|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: ARE YOU SERIOUS RIGHT NOW?|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: chat, we might have a problem here|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: get me out of this bozos cage|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: guys i might be cooked|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: bruh|
|None|[425] [YOUTUBE] im_a_squid_kid: names noted|
## UI
NOTE: UI intentionally incomplete to match its appearance in-game, where it was bugged for the duration of im_a_squid_kid's ministership.
## Trivia
- Each message sent while in prison costs 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid 7 [[Coins]] per message in each lobby, even though he is not sending them himself.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- The spoken line names noted is a reference to when 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid replied 'names noted' to 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade using his alt 1Whitelisted to spy on him during the [[The Great Potato War|Potato War]].
- The [[Potato War Silver Medal]] was given to 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid.

# Judicial Goon

The Judicial Goon was an [[NPC]] located near the [[Prison of the Bereft]]. They allowed the player to vote for or against freeing prisoners from the [[Prison of the Bereft]]. If players voted against, they gained +5 [[Magic Find]] for 2 hours.

|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Voting to free prisoners|[NPC] Goon: Fine. Just know those perks could have been yours...|
|Voting against freeing prisoners|[NPC] Goon: Smart choice. Enjoy your perks...|
|Trying to vote for freeing prisoners after claiming bribe|[NPC] Goon: Regretting your choice? Heh heh, no changing now. Come back when that bribe effect expires.|
|Trying to vote against freeing prisoners after claiming bribe|[NPC] Goon: Your greed sickens me. Wait until your current effect expires.|

# Kuudra's End
Categories: Historical content, Locations, Kuudra, Crimson Isle
Kuudra's End was significantly altered or removed after 0.16. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Kuudra's End]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Music: "Lava Kraken ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Elle]] [[Weird Sailor]]
Kuudra's End was a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Crimson Isle]], and is where the [[Kuudra]] fight took place.
## Purpose
Kuudra's End was where [[Kuudra]] spawns, and the fight took place. It was a massive space, with a bridge leading to what resembled a ship, surrounded by [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]]. [[Elle]] would fish up [[Kuudra]] and cause the bridge to collapse. [[Kuudra]] and its tentacles spawned in the [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] around the ship.

A Lantern was a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Lantern could be purchased from [[Lumina]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] for 10,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Upon right clicking, a [[Torch]] was placed. 1 [[Coal]] was consumed from the player's inventory while placing [[Torch|Torches]]. It could only be used on the [[Private Island]] or [[Crystal Hollows]] .
## Trivia
- Its texture is based on the Vanilla Lantern added in later versions of Minecraft.
- Technically, it first placed a redstone torch and then turned it into a regular torch.
  - There was a glitch where sometimes it did not turn into a regular torch.

# Lonely Island

The Lonely Island was a sub-location of [[Spruce Woods]] in [[The Park]]. It has since been entirely replaced with the [[Viking Longhouse]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Lonely Island, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Lonely Island
■ Explore the lonely structure.
## Features
The Lonely Island featured the [[Viking Longhouse]] sub-zone, which is a large structure that covered most of the Lonely Island. It is a large hall with a [[Furnace]] and fireplace, and a large wooden throne where the [[Melancholic Viking]] can be found.

# Magma Cube Boss
Aliases: Magma Cube Boss (Old)
Categories: Historical content, Manual XP Listed, Manual Other Drops, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Bosses, Blazing Fortress
Magma Cube Boss was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Magma Cube Boss]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 100
Spawn Location: [[Blazing Fortress]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 150,000; [[Damage]]: 900
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Magma Cube Pet]] 0–1x Travel Scroll to Magma Fields ([[Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull]]) 0–6x [[Ember Fragment]] 0–5x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] 0–1x [[Molten Cube]]
XP: +200 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 200
[Metadata]
ID: MAGMA_CUBE_BOSS_100
I have defeated the Magma Boss more times than I can count!
— Elle of the Nether
The Magma Cube Boss (Commonly known as Magma Boss) was a [[Bosses|BOSS]] found in the Magma Fields in the [[Blazing Fortress]].
## Spawning
The Magma Cube Boss spawned every 2 hours. The spawn time was identical in every server (a timer could be found here but the boss was revamped and now spawns every 5 minutes) (Warning timer is inaccurate as people will often report incorrect spawn and death times, the temperature mechanic was much more accurate.)
Temperature used to be a game mechanic contained in the Blazing Fortress to indicate when the boss would spawn. You could use the temperature (underneath the server info in the tab menu) to check what the current temperature was. All possible temperatures and times before spawn are described as follows:
- Warm: Less than 1 hour before the boss spawned.
- Tempered: Less than 20 minutes before the boss spawned.
- Hot: Less than 10 minutes before the boss spawned.
- Scolding: Less than 2 minutes before the boss spawn.
- Volcanic: The boss has spawned and lasted until it becomes Warm again at the 1 hour mark. If you joined a server and it says Volcanic more likely than not you missed the boss spawn and had to wait 1-2 hour(s) until the next spawn.
Prior to spawning, lava flowed down from the mountain towards the valley of the boss arena. The closer the lava is to the ground, the sooner the boss would spawn.
In addition to the lava flow, a wave of [[Blaze|Blazes]] spawned 20 minutes prior to the boss spawning. When the timer reaches 10 minutes to spawn, a wave of [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] spawned. 2 minutes prior to the boss spawning, the [[Music Discs|Music Disc]] "13" could be heard by players in the arena.
Prior to the balance update that gave the Magma Cube Boss a damage cap, If the first boss in a server was killed too quickly, there was a chance that a second and even third boss could spawn immediately.
## Drops
Each player that dealt damage to the Magma Cube Boss would have their own drops (that are only visible to them) depending on what place they received in damage dealt, similar to the [[Ender Dragon]] fight. The higher they placed, the greater the chance of getting more or rarer drops.
Total Loot Quality was calculated using this formula: Quality=BaseQuality+100*(PlayerDamageDealtFirstPlaceDamageDealt)
|Condition|Base Quality|Additional Loot|
|-|-|-|
|1st in Damage Dealt|200 Loot Quality|30 [[Magma Cream]], 7 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], 1 [[Molten Cube]]|
|2nd in Damage Dealt|175 Loot Quality|25 [[Magma Cream]], 6 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], 1 [[Molten Cube]]|
|3rd in Damage Dealt|150 Loot Quality|22 [[Magma Cream]], 5 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], 1 [[Molten Cube]]|
|4th in Damage Dealt|125 Loot Quality|20 [[Magma Cream]], 4 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], 1 [[Molten Cube]]|
|5th in Damage Dealt|110 Loot Quality|18 [[Magma Cream]], 3 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], 1 [[Molten Cube]]|
|6-8th in Damage Dealt|90 Loot Quality|15 [[Magma Cream]], 2 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], 1 [[Molten Cube]]|
|9-10th in Damage Dealt|75 Loot Quality|12 [[Magma Cream]], 2 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], 1 [[Molten Cube]]|
|11-12th in Damage Dealt|60 Loot Quality|10 [[Magma Cream]], 2 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|
|More than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|50 Loot Quality|8 [[Magma Cream]]|
|Less than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|10 Loot Quality|5 [[Magma Cream]]|
Only one unique item could drop if the player had enough quality to qualify. Rest of the quality was used on non-unique drops. The following items could've drop when killed:
|Item|Unique|Quality|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Magma Cube Pet]]|✔|200 Loot Quality|2%|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Magma Cube Pet]]|✔|150 Loot Quality|10%|
|Travel Scroll to Magma Fields ([[Dusty Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Skull]])|✔|130 Loot Quality|35%|
|[[Ember Fragment]]|✕|50 Loot Quality|100%|
|[[Magma Cream]]|✕|25 Loot Quality|100%|
|[[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|✕|5 Loot Quality|100%|
- [[Ember Rod]] (Removed after 0.7.8)
## Behavior
The Magma Boss targets and attacks nearby players, jumping to reach them and dealing heavy damage on contact. In addition, it can shoot fireballs, igniting and damaging players it hits.
The boss does not follow invincibility frames, similar to the [[Ender Dragon]]. It also bypasses protection from the [[Skeleton's Helmet]].
Any damage it receives above 2,000 is reduced to 2,000 + 10% of the additional damage.
When the boss dies, it turns the ground beneath it into yellow hardened clay, and a beacon shows its location of death. The boss's drops spawn in the vicinity of the beacon.
## Strategy
Many players should work together to efficiently defeat the boss. Since the player has a higher chance of receiving rare drops if they deal more damage than other players, many players attempt to maximize their DPS.
Another way of damaging the boss for lesser amounts of damage dealt to the player is to shoot with a bow. This will keep the player safe from the Boss's attacks but will also deal less damage than a melee weapon. Using a more powerful bow, such as [[Runaan's Bow]], would yield more damage dealt. As well as the Runaan's Bow, a Hurricane Bow was also good because of the multiple arrows being able to hit the boss at once. However, now you need late-game bows such as the [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]].
Using a melee weapon is a much riskier strategy of damaging the boss. The Magma Cube Boss would deal substantial damage to those players standing within melee range. Players were not advised to undertake this strategy unless they had very effective armor.
Using [[Potions]] also could significantly increase the amount of damage a player deals. For instance, [[Critical Potion]] can increase the player's critical damage and [[Archery Potion]] can be used to increase ranged damage.
When the boss died, each player gets separate drops, although only one player will get the [[Magma Cube Pet]]. If a player quits the lobby or exits the game when the items are dropped, they will not receive the items.
Note that [[Arrows]] often bounce off the Magma Boss due to Minecraft mechanics.
As the Magma Cube Boss is a [[Magma Cube]], the [[Slime Bow]] does 5x damage against it, meaning that it was possible to kill a Magma Boss in the first and only hit on it. It no longer works in its first phase.
### Indicators
1. Some servers did not have lava flowing correctly.
2. Some servers did not spawn the wave of blaze or magma cubes like they should.
3. The music disc could only be heard by a few players, due to a bug.
### Spawning
1. Some servers did not spawn the magma boss when they should. This was intended to be fixed in update 0.7, but it still affects certain servers.
## Trivia
- The Magma Boss was the first boss to be added, and the first to be revamped from an early-game boss to a late-game boss.
- As of one of the newer updates, the [[Skeleton's Helmet]] would not work on the boss. All bosses on the [[Crimson Isle]] bypass the [[Monster Hunter Armor]] set.
- The [[Ember Rod]] used to be a drop from the Magma Cube Boss, but was later removed, most likely because of other weapons outclassing it.
- Contrary to popular belief, you were not required to land the killing blow to have the Magma Cube Boss count toward your Bestiary.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Magma Cube Boss. The Magma Boss currently has 30,000 [[Health]].|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|The Magma Boss now has 60,000 [[Health]].|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed loot mechanics.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Now drop [[Ember Armor]] and Ember Fragments ([[Ember Fragment]]).|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Added indications of when the Boss will spawn.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Magma Bosses now always spawn at the exact same time on all Blazing Fortresses.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|+90,000 [[Health]] (from 60,000 to 150,000).|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|More monsters spawn when the Boss spawns.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added [[Magma Cube Pet]] drop.|
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Removed the [[Ember Rod]] from its possible drops.|
|May 15, 2020|Added [[Travel Scrolls]] drop.|
|Jul 1, 2020|0.7.11|Added [[Molten Cube]] drop.|
|Jul 1, 2020|0.7.11|Any hit above 2,000 damage on the Magma Boss will now deal 2,000 + 10% of the extra damage.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Fully revamped the Magma Cube Boss fight.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Changed the Magma Boss's spawn from the Magma Fields to the [[Magma Chamber]]|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|The Magma Boss now has 200,000,000 [[Health]]. See [[Magma Boss]] for more information on the change.|

# Malmar
Categories: Historical content, Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines
Malmar was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Malmar]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Location: [[Rampart's Quarry]]
Coordinates: -114 150 -24
Location: [[Aristocrat Passage]]
Coordinates: 105 151 139
Location: [[Far Reserve]]
Coordinates: -96 155 90
Malmar was an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. They sold a [[Powder Pie|Mining Pumpkin]].
## Mechanics
Malmar appeared in various locations near railroad tracks on the seventh day of each month.
## Trivia
- Malmar's spawning was often bugged, appearing when they were not supposed to and vice versa.
- Malmar was replaced with Brynmor who now sells various [[Mining]] [[Consumables]].
- Malmar has been referenced in the now renamed [[Powder Pie]] with the question what happened to him.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 20, 2024|Removed Malmar.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Malmar.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Removed Malmar.|

# Mayor Faith
Aliases: Faith
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Mayor
Mayor Faith was significantly altered or removed after September 9th 2020. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Mayor Faith]
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 7 73 -110
Mayor Faith was the placeholder [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] for Year 88. She can sometimes appear in lobbies when the correct mayor isn't loaded with [[Clerk Seraphine|Seraphine]]'s skin. Her dialogue remains unchanged.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Mayor Faith: Hello! [NPC] Mayor Faith: I'm only here to supervise the first election. [NPC] Mayor Faith: Once someone else is elected, I don't think we'll see each other again... :( [NPC] Mayor Faith: Make sure to vote!|
## Trivia
- Mayor Faith's dialogue is true, as she has never been added back after she was removed on September 9, 2020.

[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

|Sep 9, 2020|Removed Mayor Faith.|

# Minister Election
Aliases: Aura Minister, Aura Ministers
Categories: Historical content, Aura's Mayorship
Minister Election was significantly altered or removed after January 13, 2026. This article is retained for historical purposes.
The Minister Election is an election event that replaces the [[Mayor Election]] while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] is in office. It takes place in [[The People's Voting]], a room that replaced the [[Election Room]].
A player can vote on other players to become Ministers. The 7 Ministers with the most votes will be able to add their own perks to the game: the top 3 Ministers may create their own perks, whereas the remaining 4 Ministers may choose from different Mayor perks and special pre-made perks.
The player cannot vote for [[Candidate Aura|Aura]], anyone with the 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] rank, 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade, or blacklisted individuals like 1dante, 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] hannibal2, or 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon. Ministers cannot be voted in two elections in a row, unless they use the Long Term Investment perk; likewise, players who managed to reach the podium cannot be voted for in subsequent elections.
When a Minister Election concludes, the following message will display:
ATTENTION! The #th Minister Election has concluded!
Your winners are:
1st: <playername>
2nd: <playername>
3rd: <playername>
4th: <playername>
5th: <playername>
6th: <playername>
7th: <playername>

### 467th Mayor Election
In the [[Mayor Election]] that immediately followed the end of the Minister Elections, [[Aatrox]], [[Cole]], [[Foxy]], [[Diaz]], and [[Diana]] were the available candidates. All mayors had all of their perks available during this Mayor Election.
## Minister Perks
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] thirtyvirus:
  - Added the [[Precursor Drone Pet]] and [[Drone Mods|Drone Parts]]
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] MrFaceplate:
  - Added [[Potato Ring]]
  - Added [[Blood God Sigil]]
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Future77:
  - Added [[Kuudra's Kidney]], [[Kuudra's Lung]] and [[Kuudra's Heart]]
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] zj2:
  - [[Mining Fiesta]] now lasts the entire mayor duration
  - Added [[Fiesta Flask]]
  - Added [[Packrat Shard]] ([[Attributes|Fiesta Fortune]])
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Derailious:
  - Added [[Hotspot Talisman]], [[Hotspot Ring]] and [[Hotspot Artifact]]
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] fear5s:
  - Reduced the amount of rows in [[Terminal|Melody's Terminal]] from 4 -> 3
  - Reduced the amount of numbers in [[Terminal|Number's Terminal]] from 14 -> 10
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Intrests:
  - Added [[Beastslayer's Codex]]
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Dra2oon:
  - Added the [[Dead Man's Hunt]]
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] Kuvdra:
  - Added [[Eagle Pet]]
- 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Celestial_Milk:
  - Significantly reduced the time it takes for [[Catacombs]] bosses from floors 1-6 to talk
  - Significantly sped-up various actions in [[Catacombs]] F7 and M7
  - Reduced the time it takes for [[The Watcher]] to talk and spawn his summons if the floor was beaten at least 5 times
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] CandyPat:
  - Added [[Bait Sack|Small Bait Sack]], [[Bait Sack|Medium Bait Sack]] and [[Bait Sack|Large Bait Sack]] that replace [[Fishing Bag]]
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Refraction:
  - Added [[Refractive Paint Palette]]
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Alaawii:
  - Added [[Powder of One]], [[Powder of the Few]], [[Powder of the Many]] and [[Powder of the People]]
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Toadstar0:

  - Increased activation range of [[Fels]] by 5 blocks

  - Reduced purchase requirement for [[Shady Ring]] from 5 to 1 [[Dark Auction]] purchase
  - Reduced purchase requirement for [[Crooked Artifact]] from 10 to 3 [[Dark Auction]] purchases
  - Reduced purchase requirement for [[Seal of the Family]] from 15 to 7 [[Dark Auction]] purchases
  - Added [[Prosecute|Prosecute VI]] and [[Execute|Execute VI]] enchanted book to Dark Auction loot pool
  - Removed [[Sharpness|Sharpness VI]] and [[Protection|Protection VI]] enchanted book from Dark Auction loot pool
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] rembutquaglet:
  - Added [[InfiniPot™]]
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] Keep_Aura:
  - Added [[Frog's Spirit]]
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] MashClash:
  - Added pity for [[Special Zealot]]
  - Added pity for [[Vanquisher]]
  - Changed /pity to no longer require [[Booster Cookie]]
  - Changed [[Phoenix Pet]] rarities to [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] and [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]], which makes it no longer grant [[Pets|Pet Score]]
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] End_Aura:
  - Removed [[Candidate Aura]] from office after the next election
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] ALAND_:
  - Improved the [[Weather]] system
  - Added [[Professor Wynd]]
- 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] Aura_STAY:
  - [[Bestiary]] got heavily reduced
  - [[Magic Find]] rework [AHN] (This item or feature is currently only available on the Alpha Hypixel Network.)
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] FlameOfWar:
  - Reduced the amount of time it takes to pick up [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]] from 11 ticks to 5
  - Reduced the amount of lights in [[Terminal|Simon Says Terminal]] by 1
## Notes
- If, because of ministers, a same perk would be activated at the same time — for example due to Perkpocalypse — its effect would not stack with itself and only be active once.
## Trivia
- 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] hannibal2 was blacklisted from being able to be voted for after a developer of the popular mod SkyHanni auto-filled the sign where players could vote on a minister with hannibal2, which counted as a chat macro: the developer behind this was also temporarily banned.
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid, 4[[Ranks|[MVP]]] Confused_Goon and 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] SnowflakeKiwi chose to use their perks to remove the Minion Union, Work Harder and Work Smarter perks, respectively. As a result, they were imprisoned in the [[Prison of the Bereft]] and replaced with an [[Electoral Goon]] for the duration of their term.
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] AscentPVP was disqualified from the fifth election for bribing people with giveaways and invited to campaign again under the pretense that no money would be involved.
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Future77's Organ Donor perk is a reference to a running gag where Future77 jokes about selling his organs to afford the coins to 'hypermax' items in SkyBlock.
- While the Work Harder perk affects the [[Health]] values shown in [[Bestiary]] of mobs, it doesn't change the [[Damage]] value, which is also increased by the perk.
  - [[Derpy]]'s DOUBLE MOBS HP!!! doesn't change values in Bestiary.
  - Slayer menu shows both increases to Boss's [[Health]], but omits the [[Damage]].
- After the [[Precursor Drone Pet]], [[Kuudra Tentacle]], [[Blood God Sigil]], and [[Potato Ring]] were added, new items stopped being added after the conclusion of each individual Minister Election. Instead, they were slowly rolled out over time and eventually in one major update.
- 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] arithemonkey's perk, which was to add a 2nd [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]] to the game, was implemented on March 29th, 2026: 3 days before the beginning of April.
- 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Derailious's perk was originally the Magical Milk Mixin, however with the implementation of [[Celestial Mason Jar]] which granted the same perk, his perk was changed.
- 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Luamon's perk, which was implemented as part of the [[Loadouts]] system, was first seen on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] on June 24th, 2026.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 28, 2025|Added Minister Election.|
|Jan 12, 2026|Ended the Minister Election.|

# Night Squid
Categories: Historical content, Sea Creatures, Mobs without Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs
Night Squid was significantly altered or removed after 0.25. This article is retained for historical purposes.
Pitch darkness reveals a Night Squid.
[Night Squid]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 6
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 4000
[Fishing Info]
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 1100
Fishing Location: Anywhere ([[Sea Creatures]])
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing III (3) ([[Fishing]])
Catch Times: 7:00 PM – 6:00 AM
[Drops]
Drops: 6–17x [[Ink Sac]] 1x [[Lily Pad]] 0–1x RARE Fishing Exp Boost ([[Fishing Exp Boost]]) 0–1x UNCOMMON Fishing Exp Boost ([[Fishing Exp Boost]]) 0–1x [[Squid Boots]]
XP: +270 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 10
[Metadata]
ID: NIGHT_SQUID_6
The Night Squid was a [[Rarity|COMMON]] variant of the [[Squid]] found only at night while using [[Dark Bait]] or fishing in [[The Park]] during rain. It required [[Fishing|Fishing III (3)]] to fish up.
## Spawning
A Night Squid could be fished up from any source of [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] during Nighttime while using [[Dark Bait]] if the player had [[Fishing|Fishing III (3)]] or higher.
However, while the rain bought from [[Vanessa]] is active in [[The Park]], the catching chance for the Night Squid would become a set value, making the chance for a player's sea creature catch to be a Night Squid 22.2%. Importantly, during rain, Night Squid can still be rolled normally if the necessary conditions are met (Nighttime, [[Dark Bait]] and [[Fishing|Fishing III (3)]] or higher).
## Weight
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Night Squid was 1100. Certain modifiers could be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Common Hook]]|1.25x|
|[[Squid Hat]]|2x|

## Tips
- The Night Squid was the best [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] to farm [[Ink Sac|Ink Sacs]] from as it dropped more [[Ink Sac|Ink Sacs]] than any other Sea Creature.
- The maximum possible Night Squid rate was 31.10%; obtainable with rain from [[Vanessa]], [[Common Hook]], and [[Squid Hat]] during Nighttime with [[Dark Bait]].

# Official Buildings
Categories: Historical content, Official Buildings, Locations
Official Buildings was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
While [[Dante]] was elected, some buildings on the [[Hub Island]] were classified as Official Buildings. These buildings were accessible by wearing the [[Happy Mask]], or by holding the [[Dante's Talisman]] either in your inventory or the [[Accessory Bag]]. The buildings were guarded by [[Goons]], who blocked the entrance and pushed the player back, if they were not wearing the [[Happy Mask]] or had the [[Dante's Talisman]] active.
## Buildings
The Official Buildings included the following:
- [[Bank]]
- [[Farmhouse]]
- [[Auction House]]
- [[Bazaar Alley]]
## Trivia
- The Auction House and Bazaar could still be accessed without the Happy Mask or Dante's Talisman if the player used the commands available after consuming the [[Booster Cookie]].
- If a player entered the [[Bazaar Alley]] from the side or back entrance without a [[Happy Mask]] or a [[Dante's Talisman]], the player would be teleported back to the entrance of the area.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 4, 2021|Added Official Buildings.|
|Apr 16, 2021|Official Buildings categorization no longer used.|

# Operation Mayhem
Aliases: Agents of Operation Mayhem
Categories: Historical content, Quests
Operation Mayhem was significantly altered or removed after November 30, 2022. This article is retained for historical purposes.
Agent Kapustin outside of Operation Mayhem
Operation Mayhem was going to be a quest and a minigame found in the [[Jerry's Workshop]] event. During its existence, it remained "Closed for Maintenance", until it was replaced with the [[Glacial Cave]]. The Agents of Operation Mayhem still talk about it, though are now ice miners inside the cave. Operation Mayhem was supposed to be a snowball fight inside of the [[Snowball Fight Cave|Snowball Fight Caves]].

# Pigman
Aliases: Zombie Pigman, Zombie Pigmen, Zombie pigmen
Categories: Historical content, Vanilla objects, Pages with broken file links, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Blazing Fortress
Pigman was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
Pigman is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Zombie Pigman.
[A sprite icon representing Pigman in Hypixel SkyBlock Pigman]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 12
Spawn Location: [[Blazing Fortress]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 240; [[Damage]]: 125
[Drops]

XP: +15 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 25
[Metadata]
ID: PIGMAN_12
Pigmen, which are also known as Zombie Pigmen, were neutral mobs that spawned in the [[Blazing Fortress]]. They do not serve as a base for any [[Collections]], but they drop [[Gold Nugget|Gold Nuggets]]. They are neutral as long as players don't provoke them. If one player attacks any Pigman, they will all become aggressive to the attacking player. Pigmen in the [[Crimson Isle]] will still be provoked even if the player travels to a different map and then back to the Crimson Isle. Pigman [[Bestiary]] can still be obtained by killing the Pigmen on [[Magma Cube Rider|Magma Cube Riders]].
This mob had 240 [[Health]] and dealt 125 [[Damage]].

# Potato King
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Great Potato War
Potato King was significantly altered or removed after June 30, 2020. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Potato King]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 8 70 -104
The Potato King was an [[NPC]] located near the [[Map (Hub)|Map]], granting talismans and special items. The following items were available at no cost:
- [[Potato Talisman]], granted to all players.
- [[Basket of Hope from the Great Potato War]], granted to players in the top 1,000 [[Potato|Potato Collection]].
- [[Potato Crown]], granted to the winner of the Great Potato War (8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade).
- [[Potato War Silver Medal]], granted to the second-highest potato collection (7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid).
## Trivia
- The Potato King's skin is based on the YouTuber 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade. He is seen holding a giant potato. He was the first YouTube personality to have an in-game NPC, with thirtyvirus, TimeDeo, FurryEBoy, 56ms, and Refraction all receiving their own as [[Resistance General|Generals]]. Technoblade also received another NPC as the [[Resistance Leader]], making him the only YouTube personality to have multiple in-game NPCs.
- The Potato King was added as a reward for Technoblade winning [[The Great Potato War]].
- The NPC was supposed to be up for only 24 hours, but was made to stay until the end of Friday, June 26, 2020 by the Hypixel team "to make sure nobody misses out" . However, the NPC remained until the following Tuesday, June 30, 2020, when it was removed.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 22, 2020|Added Potato King to the Hub.|
|Jun 30, 2020|The Potato King is removed from the Hub.|
Linked from: [[King (disambiguation)]], [[Pig (disambiguation)]], [[Potato]], [[Potato (disambiguation)]], [[Potato Talisman]], [[Potato War Silver Medal]], [[The Great Potato War]]

# Prison of the Bereft
Aliases: Prison Of The Bereft
Categories: Historical content, Locations, Aura's Mayorship
Prison of the Bereft was significantly altered or removed after January 19, 2026. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Prison of the Bereft]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Hub Island]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): Confused Goon ([[Confused Goon (player)]]) im_a_squid_kid ([[Im a squid kid]]) [[SnowflakeKiwi]]
The Prison of the Bereft was a location found on the [[Hub Island]] while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] was in office. It was a metal cage where players were sent to for a duration of 5 minutes for committing various offenses.
## Game Message
When a player was sent to prison, a message appeared for everyone in the lobby:
JAILED! <player> has been sent to jail!
Players were sent into the cage by doing one of the following:
- Punching a [[Goons|Goon]]
- Using the /killaura command
- Sending "Aura Sucks", "Aura Bad", "Screw Aura", or similar in the chat (includes profanity)
When sent to the Prison of the Bereft, the following text displayed:
  INSOLENT! You have been sent to jail for 5m.
While in prison, the player couldn't warp anywhere and the /killaura command was disabled. Additionally, throwing [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] and summoning [[Pets]] were disabled.
## Prison Break
After the first [[Minister Election]] ended, 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] im_a_squid_kid was placed in jail instead of becoming Minister. A [[Corrupt Goon]] appeared in the basement of the [[Hexatorum]] to sell a [[Rusty Spoon]] that could be used to participate in breaking the cage. Once the Prison of the Bereft was broken, players could no longer be jailed.
## Prison Reconstruction
At the end of the second [[Minister Election]], the Prison of the Bereft was repaired. It swapped from wood to metal and placed 3 [[Guard Goon|Guard Goons]] and a [[Judicial Goon]] around it. This allowed players to be jailed again; in addition, 4[[Ranks|[MVP]]] Confused_Goon was placed in the jail.
Following the eighth [[Minister Election]], [[SnowflakeKiwi]] was imprisoned for attempting to remove the Work Smarter perk.
At the ninth and last [[Minister Election]], all methods to be imprisoned were disabled.
Following [[Candidate Aura|Aura]]'s leave from office, the Prison of the Bereft was removed.
## Trivia
- Once [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] was elected, a [[Goons|Goon]] was seen building the cage block by block until completion.
- If a player attempted any of the actions that sent them to jail again while their sentence wasn't over, they were sent back to jail and the timer reset.
- It was possible to escape the Prison of the Bereft in multiple ways, such as using an [[Ender Pearl]] or [[Horse Pet]]/ [[Skeleton Horse Pet]]. However, the game still classified the player as imprisoned. If the player warped away from the [[Hub Island]], they were sent back shortly after.
- [[Minister Election|Ministers]] that betrayed [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] were imprisoned for the duration of their term.
- While [[Aura]] and her [[Goons]] were present on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], the Prison of the Bereft was not.
- Bereft means 'lacking in something'.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 28, 2025|Added Prison of the Bereft.|
|Dec 2, 2025|Fixed players being able to glitch out of the Prison of the Bereft.|
|Dec 4, 2025|Players no longer are sent to the Prison of the Bereft.|
|Dec 7, 2025|Repaired the Prison of the Bereft.|
|Dec 7, 2025|Players can be jailed again.|
|Dec 7, 2025|Added Guard Goons ([[Guard Goon]]) and the [[Judicial Goon]] around the Prison of the Bereft.|
|Dec 12, 2025|Removed Guard Goons ([[Guard Goon]]) and the [[Judicial Goon]] around the Prison of the Bereft.|
|Jan 13, 2026|Players no longer are sent to the Prison of the Bereft.|
|Jan 19, 2026|Removed the Prison of the Bereft.|
Linked from: [[Confused Goon]], [[Confused Goon (player)]], [[Goons]], [[Guard Goon]], [[Im a squid kid]], [[Judicial Goon]], [[Minister Election]], [[Rusty Spoon]], [[SnowflakeKiwi]]

# Resistance Movement
Aliases: Resistance, The Resistance, The Uprising, Uprising
Categories: Historical content, Pages with UIs, The Uprising
Resistance Movement was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
The leaders of the resistance: Technoblade and his generals (From left to right: 56ms, TimeDeo, thirtyvirus, 2nfg, FurryEBoy, Refraction) & Seraphine
When [[Dante]] was elected mayor, [[Clerk Seraphine|Seraphine]] created a Resistance Movement in the [[Dark Auction]] house. This led to the election of the [[Resistance Leader]] (8Technoblade) and his generals. The revolt happened on April 16, 2021 at 6:30PM UTC.
## Dante's message in the Hub
After the popular Hypixel YouTuber Technoblade went live about the Resistance Movement, a tweet from Hypixel's official Twitter, revealed a message from Dante. This message replaced the map in the [[Hub Island|Hub]]. Click here to see Dante's Message
## Resistance Generals
The other six elected were the runner-ups in the election. They served as Technoblade's [[Resistance General|generals]] during the fight against Dante. Since 2nfg was banned at the time of the Uprising, he did not become a Minister, unlike the other five. However, his perk of reducing the player's Intelligence by 1 is still provided.
## Information
On 4/12/2021, popular Minecraft youtuber 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade was elected the leader of the resistance. Alongside him, the next six players with the most votes, were elected generals (756ms, 12TimeDeo, 7thirtyvirus, 52nfg, 7FurryEBoy, 7Refraction), who led the Resistance against [[Dante]].
- Special armor, called [[Armor of the Rising Sun]] was released, as well as a new weapon, [[Staff of the Rising Sun]], which was the only weapon that could deal damage to Dante. In order to obtain this armor from Seraphine, the player must've had at least 8 hours of playtime. Voting was not a requirement.
## Boss Fight
See [[Dante|Dante Uprising Fight]].
## The Uprising
Banner of the Uprising (Rising Sun)
The Uprising is the second half of the Resistance Movement, which involves the [[Armor of the Rising Sun]] and [[Staff of the Rising Sun]] distribution, the [[Dante|Dante Uprising Fight]] and the Post-Uprising period. Mayor [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] and [[Resistance General|Resistance Generals]]' perks were applied during the Post-Uprising period.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 6, 2021|Added Resistance Movement (officially called "the resistance")|
|Apr 7, 2021|Map in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]) was replaced by a message from Dante about the resistance|
|Apr 12, 2021|Resistance voting was finished, and Technoblade was elected the leader of the Resistance|
|Apr 15, 2021|The uprising date was announced and could be checked by interacting with Seraphine ([[Clerk Seraphine]])|
|Apr 16, 2021|The uprising happened on April 16, 2021 at 6:30PM UTC|
Linked from: [[Armor of the Rising Sun]], [[Blood God Crest]], [[Blood God Shrine]], [[Changelog]], [[Clerk Seraphine]], [[Collectable Items]], [[Community Center]], [[Dante]], [[Dante/Dialogue]], [[Dark Auction]], [[Goon (Mob)]], [[Goons]], [[Graveyard]], [[Health]], [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]], [[Intelligence]], [[Mayor Election/Events]], [[Museum]], [[Museum/Items]], [[Museum/Items/Special]], [[Pig (disambiguation)]], [[Pig Mask]], [[Piggy]], [[Rat Pet]], [[Relic (disambiguation)]], [[Resistance General]], [[Resistance Leader]], [[Shiny Token]], [[Slime (disambiguation)]], [[Slime Goon]], [[Spiked Atrocity]]

# SnowflakeKiwi
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Aura's Mayorship
SnowflakeKiwi was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[SnowflakeKiwi]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Prison of the Bereft]]
SnowflakeKiwi is an [[NPC]] in the [[Prison of the Bereft]] based on a streamer with the same name.
SnowflakeKiwi was elected as a [[Minister Election|Minister]] to [[Candidate Aura|Aura]]. When she won, she attempted to remove the Work Smarter perk and was sent to the [[Prison of the Bereft]]. While imprisoned, she walks around the cage and sends messages in chat.
## Dialogue

|-|-|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: Hot Meowers|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: I don't like the rain|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: hey can you guys get me out of here pls? thanks|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: wheres the exit? i think i lost the key|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: Fun Fact: Elephants dont wear socks|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: absolute cinema|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: @hypixel pls get me out|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: SOMEBODY DO SOMETHING!|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: ARE YOU SERIOUS RIGHT NOW?|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: chat, we might have a problem here|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: guys i might be cooked|
|None|[292] [MVP++] SnowflakeKiwi: bruh|
## Trivia
- SnowflakeKiwi rebranded to NetworkIssue since being elected. Her previous username is kept for historical purposes.
- SnowflakeKiwi appears to share [[Im a squid kid|im_a_squid_kid]]'s dialogue, with a few additional lines of her own.

## Obtaining
Sunder I could be purchased from the [[The Garden|SkyMart]] in [[The Garden]] for 10 [[Copper]].

Sunder VI had a 0.5% chance of dropping upon killing a [[Locust]] [[Pests|Pest]] in [[The Garden]].
## Ability
Sunder granted up to +75 [[Farming Fortune]], which increases your chance for multiple crops.

|Ⅴ|+62.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|
|Ⅵ|+75 [[Farming Fortune]]|
## Applying
Sunder could be applied to any [[Axes|Axe]]. It required [[Enchanting|Enchanting II (2)]] to apply.

Surveillance Goons were [[NPC|NPCs]] located near the entrance of every island when the Public Surveillance perk is chosen by an elected minister when [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] was mayor. They were initially introduced as part of 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Candypat's Public Surveillance perk and required players to check in every 30 minutes. Failing to do so granted a 10% stat debuff. The duration was later increased to 12 hours and added as part of 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] MelonKingDe's Minister perks.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Checking in|[NPC] Goon: Aura thanks you for your cooperation. Come back in 30 minutes if you do not want to face the consequences.|
|Interacting right after checking in|[NPC] Goon: You've just checked in a few seconds ago... [NPC] Goon: Now go, be a productive citizen for our great society.|
## Trivia
- Surveillance Goon can appear both with and without a [[Stick]] in hand, seemingly at random.
## Gallery
- Holding a stick

# Telekinesis (Enchantment)
Categories: Historical content, Enchantments
Telekinesis (Enchantment) was significantly altered or removed after 2022/Apr 25. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Telekinesis (Enchantment)]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Maximum Level: I
Applied To: [[Tools]], [[Weapons]], [[Fishing Rods]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_TELEKINESIS
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Telekinesis I Block and mob drops go directly into your inventory.; Apply Cost: 5 Exp Levels; COMMON
Telekinesis was an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that made all block and mob drops appear instantly in the inventory. It could be applied to [[Tools]], [[Weapons]] and [[Fishing Rods]].
## Obtaining
The Telekinesis [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] could be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Telekinesis I|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Telekinesis I|Applied at [[Rusty]] for 100 [[Coins]]|
|Telekinesis I|Drop from [[Ender Nodes]]|
## Ability
Block and Mob drops go directly into players inventory.
## Applying
The Telekinesis [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] could be applied to [[Tools]], [[Weapons]] and [[Fishing Rods]].
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|5 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- After Telekinesis was removed, every [[Enchanted Book]] (Telekinesis) was replaced with blank [[Enchanted Book]] with [[Rarity|COMMON]] rarity.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Added Telekinesis I.|
|Jul 11, 2019|Fixed Telekinesis not being applicable on [[Bows]].|
|Jul 11, 2019|Telekinesis no longer works on [[Bosses]].|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|[[Rusty]] can now apply Telekinesis I.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Telekinesis I can now drop from [[Ender Nodes]].|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed Telekinesis not working on custom blocks, droppers, furnaces and hoppers.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed Telekinesis drop sometimes not stacking.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed rare drops message not working with Telekinesis.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed 0 [[Coins]] showing when killing Animals ([[Animal]]) with Telekinesis.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed some item [[Quests]] not working with Telekinesis.|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Telekinesis can now be applied to [[Fishing Rods]].|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Telekinesis now works with Experience Orbs ([[Experience]]) on [[Swords]].|
|May 24, 2020|Fix [[Quiver]] changes not being applied to Telekinesis.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Fixed Telekinesis only working on bottom block of [[Sugar Cane]] and [[Cactus]] on the [[Private Island]].|
|Jul 30, 2020|Telekinesis now works on [[Unincorporated]] mobs.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Telekinesis now works on top half of doors.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Telekinesis now works on extra [[Seeds]] from [[Rookie Hoe]].|
|Jul 27, 2021|Fixed Telekinesis dropping [[Cobblestone]] instead of [[Hard Stone]] while riding an [[Armadillo Pet]].|
|Apr 25, 2022|Replaced Telekinesis with [[Auto-pickup]].|
|Aug 1, 2022|Fixed Telekinesis still appearing in the Enchantment Guide ([[Enchantment Nerd]]).|

# The Catacombs - Floor IX
Categories: Historical content, Catacombs, Dungeons
The Catacombs - Floor IX was significantly altered or removed after v0.9.13. This article is retained for historical purposes.
The Catacombs - Floor IX was the ninth floor in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] Dungeon with [[Bosses|BOSS]] [[Storm]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] [[The Watcher]]. This boss was described in game as follows "Graced with tremendous abilities, earned the long forgotten title of Elementalist after the destruction of an entire land."
As of SkyBlock Update 0.9.13, this has been removed.
Linked from: [[Storm]], [[The Catacombs - Floor VIII]]

# The Catacombs - Floor VIII
Categories: Historical content, Catacombs, Dungeons
The Catacombs - Floor VIII was significantly altered or removed after v0.9.13. This article is retained for historical purposes.
The Catacombs - Floor VIII was the eighth floor in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] Dungeon with [[Bosses|BOSS]] [[Goldor]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] [[The Watcher]]. This boss was described in game as "Official title is Captain, but calls himself Soldier because it sounds better. Battle veteran."
As of SkyBlock Update 0.9.13, Floor 8, [[The Catacombs - Floor IX|9]], and [[The Catacombs - Floor X|10]] have been removed.
Linked from: [[Goldor]]

# The Catacombs - Floor X
Aliases: The Catacombs - Floor 10
Categories: Historical content, Catacombs, Dungeons
The Catacombs - Floor X was significantly altered or removed after v0.9.13. This article is retained for historical purposes.
The Catacombs - Floor X was the final floor in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] Dungeon with FINAL-BOSS [[Necron]]. This boss is described in game as follows "Right hand of the Wither King. Inherited the Catacombs eons ago. never defeated, feared by anything living AND dead." but appears on Floor VII rather than Floor X.
As of SkyBlock Update 0.9.13, this has been removed.
Linked from: [[The Catacombs - Floor VIII]]

# The Lost Adventurer
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters
The Lost Adventurer was significantly altered or removed after 0.16. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[The Lost Adventurer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Hub Island]]
The Lost Adventurer was an NPC found in various locations around the [[Hub Island]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] The Lost Adventurer: What...? Where...? Why...? [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: <player>, you there! Where am I? Select an option: [The Hub Island?]|
|Selecting [The Hub Island?]|[NPC] The Lost Adventurer: What is... 'The Hub Island'? [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: I seem to be very lost... [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: Can you help me find my way home? Select an option: [Yes...]|
|Selecting [Yes...]|[NPC] The Lost Adventurer: I can feel my home so close, it's here... and yet... it's not. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: It feels like yesterday, but perhaps... it has been longer. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: You seem smart... where should I go to find my home?|
|After completing dialogue without having reached Combat XII (12) ([[Combat]])|[NPC] The Lost Adventurer: As smart as you seem, you are not enough of a skilled warrior. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: I need you to help me find my home, however dangerous it may be. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: Reach Combat Level XII and come help me again!|
|After completing dialogue|[NPC] The Lost Adventurer: Please tell me where I can go to find my home! Select an option: [The High Level] [The End Island]|
|Selecting [The High Level]|[NPC] The Lost Adventurer: So if you keep bugging me you think I'll tell you everything? [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: Well it's working... [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: You might be getting close to knowing the truth!|
|Selecting [The End Island]|[NPC] The Lost Adventurer: I would like to start growing stronger by killing some of those spiders. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: But I've always hated flying through the air. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: I've heard stepping on those launch pads will send me flying. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer:It sends shivers down my spine. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: I think I am just going to have to learn a different way over there. [NPC] The Lost Adventurer: Have you heard of anything I can use?|
## Trivia
- Learning a way home... was displayed over The Lost Adventurer's head.
- The Lost Adventurer was the only NPC to have its dialogue and location changed so often.
- The Lost Adventurer was the only NPC with a [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level]] above their head.
  - The NPC's level was [82] while at [[Coordinates|-94, 75, -132]].
  - The NPC's level was [127] while at [[Coordinates|-156.5, 76, -169.5]].
- The Lost Adventurer 'found their way home' and was replaced by the [[Lone Adventurer]] in [[The End]] once the island was revamped.
  - The [[NPC]] had been teasing an update to the island throughout its existence.
## Gallery
- The Lost Adventurer's original appearance on top of the Community Center The Lost Adventurer's original appearance on top of the [[Community Center]]
- The Lost Adventurer's first outfit, in the Auction House The Lost Adventurer's first outfit, in the [[Auction House]]
- The Lost Adventurer's second outfit, in the Tavern The Lost Adventurer's second outfit, in the [[Tavern]]
- The Lost Adventurer's third outfit, in the Graveyard The Lost Adventurer's third outfit, in the [[Graveyard]]
- The Lost Adventurer's fourth and final outfit, in the Graveyard The Lost Adventurer's fourth and final outfit, in the [[Graveyard]]

|Oct 13, 2022|AHN 0.16|Removed The Lost Adventurer.|

# The People's Voting
Categories: Historical content, Locations, Aura's Mayorship
The People's Voting was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[The People's Voting]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 42 42 -117
The People's Voting replaced the [[Election Room]] while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] was mayor. It housed the [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]], which replaced the [[Mayor Election]] event during [[Candidate Aura|Aura]]'s mayorship.
## Functions
The People's Voting functioned similarly to the [[Election Room]]. When open, players could enter to vote for the 7 [[Minister Election|ministers]] while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] was mayor. Once the election was over, The People's Voting closed until the next voting period.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 28, 2025|Added The People's Voting.|
|Jan 15, 2026|Removed The People's Voting.|
Linked from: [[Minister Election]]

# Thunder (event)
Categories: Historical content, The Uprising
Thunder (event) was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
A storm has begun bringing along some rare empowered creatures!
  Not to be confused with the event in the [[Spider's Den]], [[Spider's Den|Thunderstorm]].
Thunder or Storm is an [[Ancient Pig]] buff that lasts for 20 minutes. During the buff, it begins to rain, thunder strikes in random locations, and [[Charged Creeper|Charged Creepers]] begin to spawn. Very rarely a [[Skeleton Horse]] can spawn too.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 16, 2021|Added Thunder.|
|May 7, 2021|The [[Ancient Pig]] is summoned, so the Thunder event is now available.|
|May 9, 2021|The [[Ancient Pig]] is disappears, so the Thunder event is no longer available.|
Linked from: [[Animal]], [[Arcane]], [[Charged Creeper]], [[Cubic (Mob Type)]], [[Mob Types/List/Animal]], [[Mob Types/List/Arcane]], [[Mob Types/List/Cubic]], [[Mob Types/List/Skeletal]], [[Skeletal]], [[Skeleton Horse]], [[Weather]]

# Warren
Categories: Historical content, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
Warren was significantly altered or removed after May 19th, 2020. This article is retained for historical purposes.
Warren is going on vacation for a long long time.
— 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon
[Warren]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Bazaar Alley]]
Coordinates: 37 71 -85
Warren was an [[NPC]] located on the [[Bazaar Alley]]. Warren used to sell [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonks]] for 200 [[Coins]] each; however, this was discontinued after only an hour after the 0.7.7 update.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Shop
|Icon|Type|Price|
|-|-|-|
|[[Stock of Stonks]]|[[Stock of Stonks]]|200 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Warren was the only [[NPC]] that had their item selling ability removed.
- Warren was meant to buy [[Stock of Stonks]], as his GUI suggests, however, this feature was never implemented ("Warren isn't buying stocks yet!").
- According to May 19, 2020, Warren is going on vacation for a long long time.
  - That could mean there is a possibility of Warren getting re-added in the future, or could mean that it is never intended for him to be added again, depending on how people look at it.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 9, 2020|0.7.7|Added Warren.|
|Mar 9, 2020|0.7.7|Warren can no longer sell [[Stock of Stonks]].|
|May 19, 2020|Removed Warren.|
Linked from: [[Bazaar Alley]], [[Changelog]], [[Changelog/2020/May 19]], [[Ghost of Warren]], [[Stock of Stonks]]

NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Weaponsmith

■ Purchase Starter Gear and train with Rosetta.

## This Month's Featured Article
This is the current Article of the Month, written by User:AetherSword. See how you can write articles for this column.
It's Spooky Month!
Spooky time is approaching, a [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]] even!
What is the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] brewing? and why is he scared of the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]]?
Help him gather ingredients for his brew, he will reward you with various items that will increase your... [[Fear]]??
The more [[Fear]] you have, the faster you spawn your [[Primal Fear|Primal Fears]] every time you kill a mob, and if you try and kill them they will transform into very scary things, such as commitment, darkness, speaking in public... and MATH!!!
Successfully dealing with your [[Primal Fear|Primal Fears]] will reward you with [[Dark Candy|Dark Candies]], which can be donated to [[Vargul the Unearthed]], a giant zombie located in his giant grave behind the mountain of the Hub.
Donating [[Dark Candy|Dark Candies]] can be done in exchange for various items, and with enough candies donated, a unique [[SkyBlock Levels|Emblem]] is rewarded!
It's time to get spooked!

# SkyBlock
Aliases: About SkyBlock, Hypixel SkyBlock, Skyblock
Categories: Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki
Play with friends (or solo!), build your private islands and collect all the items!
— Hypixel's Developers, description of SkyBlock
The default SkyBlock island every player starts out on
Hypixel SkyBlock, or simply SkyBlock, is a persistent minigame released in the [[Hypixel|Hypixel Network]] on June 11, 2019. It can be accessed by clicking on the NPC in the Prototype lobby, by pressing on the head icon in the server selector menu, or by executing the command /play skyblock, /play sb, or /skyblock. SkyBlock is one of the most popular games on Hypixel.
SkyBlock generally aims to support the most recent two Minecraft game drops. As of August 18, 2026, Minecraft version 26.1 or above is required to play SkyBlock.
## Overview
SkyBlock is commonly referred to as an MMORPG (Massively Multiplayer Online Role–Playing Game), therefore, has no win condition or specific goal. Rather, players create their own goals: some attempt to get the best [[Armor]] and [[Weapons]], some work to maximize their [[Skills]], and some build impressive islands. Players start on the [[Private Island]], and are given [[Quests]] that teach them how to play. When players go through the portal to the [[Hub Island|Hub]], they have many options. They can start leveling [[Skills]], such as [[Mining]], [[Farming]], and [[Combat]]. There are separate islands focused on specific skills, unlocked after a certain amount of levels in that skill. Leveling these skills up increases various [[Stats|statistics]], including [[Health]], [[Crit Damage]], and [[Defense]]. Along with these skills, players level their [[Collections]], which are focused on a specific item, like [[Rotten Flesh]], [[Cobblestone]], and [[Potato|Potatoes]]. These collections all grant skill experience towards the related skill (for example, [[Potato|Potatoes]] give [[Farming]] experience). Most collections have a [[Minions|minion]], which keep collecting that specific item(s) even while the player is offline.
## Skills
There are thirteen Skills in Hypixel SkyBlock, which are automatically leveled up based on the actions performed by a player.
In order to gain XP for a specific skill, players need to perform tasks corresponding to a skill. For example, to level up [[Mining]], the player would need to mine blocks. Players can also level up their skills by collecting resources from their minions.
Leveling up skills provides rewards and also permanently increases various player [[Stats]]. For example, leveling up [[Farming]] gives the player coins and increases their [[Health]].
[[Fishing]], [[Alchemy]], [[Hunting]], [[Carpentry]], and [[Dungeoneering]] go from level 1-50; [[Foraging]] goes up to 57; [[Runecrafting]] and [[Social]] go up to 25; [[Farming]], [[Mining]], [[Enchanting]], and [[Combat]] go up to 60. Level 1 requires 50 XP to obtain, meaning the default level is Level 0.
The player's skill level can be seen by opening the [[SkyBlock Menu]] and going to "Skills" (displayed as a [[Diamond Sword]]).
## Collections
Collections are a way of unlocking new crafting recipes, item [[Trades]] or receiving rewards for collecting a certain item (such as skill XP). Most vanilla items have their own collection; the more of that item the player collects, the further the player will progress in its collection. The further the player is in a collection, the more levels that will be reached, unlocking more recipes and rewards. Note that items bought from the [[Bazaar]] or [[Auction House]] and items dropped or placed by players, do not count; however items collected from [[Minions]] do. There are boss collections, which increase by killing the respective bosses. Every collection gives the player an item related to the boss or an item dropped by the boss (e.g. [[Bonzo's Staff|Bonzo Staff]], which is dropped by [[Bonzo]], can be obtained through the Bonzo Collection for free after killing Bonzo 150 times)
To access collections, open the [[SkyBlock Menu]] and click the painting named "Collections." Then, click on a skill to reveal its item collections. The skills that contain collections are [[Farming]], [[Mining]], [[Combat]], [[Foraging]], [[Fishing]], and [[Bosses|Boss]].
Each item collection will show a row of glass panes showing the player's progress on collecting the items. Green panes indicate completed, yellow indicates progress is being made towards this level, and red indicates no progress has been made towards this level. Clicking on a pane will show the rewards for the tier.
- Collection menu icon
- Farming collection
- Cactus collection
- Cactus progress
There are currently 86 item collections, 7 Dungeon boss collections, and [[Kuudra|1 non-Dungeon boss collection]].
## Minions
Minions are a fundamental part of Hypixel SkyBlock. They allow the player to earn resources even while offline. A Minion generates resources from the center of a 5x5 Area by default and generates different materials depending on its type. They cannot be sold or added to the Auction House, but they can be traded. Minions can also be upgraded, up to tier XI (or tier XII for mining and farming minions), with each tier increasing either minion capacity or decreasing action time. Players will have a Tier I Cobblestone minion on their [[Private Island|starter island]] when they first spawn.
- Coal Minion IV
- Cobblestone Minion I Menu
- Cobblestone Minion Layout (White glass represents air)
Minions can be crafted into higher tiers which increases their collection speed and its maximum storage size, with tier XI/XII being the highest tier. Tiered minions can be distinguished by their colored patch on their heads, with light green patches being tier II-IV, purple patches being tier V-VII, orange patches being tier VII-X, and cyan patches being tier XI. Also, there is a quick crafting diamond, so that the owner does not need to manually craft the next tier.
Initially, the player can only place five minions. 27 minion slots are unlocked by crafting various unique minion tiers. You can gain an additional 5 minion slots through [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Profile Upgrades]]. This means players can have a total of 32 minion slots. (see [[Minions|increasing minion capacity]]).
## Locations
Locations are a fundamental part of Hypixel SkyBlock, and they are where players collect items for [[Collections]], interact with [[NPC|NPCs]], grind skills or more.
## NPCs
NPCs, or Non-Player Characters, are characters that are scattered around the map. You can talk to them, and some of them will have a [[Quests|Quest]] for you, or own a [[Shops|Shop]] where you are able to purchase items for a price. Each Area has at least one NPC. Some NPCs sell items while others might sell Weapons/Tools that you can't craft. For NPC that do sell items, you buy those items and, sell them higher than their buy price. For more information check [[Bazaar|NPC Flipping]].
## Quests
The quest log
Quests is a gameplay mechanic that allows new players to explore the world and earn rewards for doing tasks. Different quests have different tasks that can require players to collect or find certain items or explore an area.
Note: This page will contain several spoilers on how to complete each quest. It is ordered in the recommended order of completion, although players are free to complete these in any order they prefer.
To find [[NPC|NPCs]], press F3 on the keyboard and use the coordinates listed after the XYZ. Also, if one's inventory ever becomes full, remember to utilize the personal ender chest found in the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. These quests were completed on an alternate account and not by guesswork so every quest mentioned in this guide should be possible.
## Achievements
The achievements button
Achievements are in-game goals that the player can accomplish and award achievement points for unlocking cosmetic features on the Hypixel server game lobbies (not SkyBlock Lobbies). In the [[Changelog|July 2nd Update (2019)]], 60 achievements were added.
Players can view their achievement progress by opening the [[SkyBlock Menu]], clicking "My Hypixel Profile (one square up from om left), clicking "Achievements" (diamond icon), and then clicking the globe icon. Please note that as Hypixel adds achievements, actions that the player has completed might show as uncompleted; if this occurs simply try and repeat this action again to complete the achievement.
## Trades
Trades (not to be confused with [[Trading]]) are a SkyBlock Mechanic where players can trade items in their inventory for other items supplied by the server, using a submenu in their [[SkyBlock Menu]]. Trades can only be unlocked through a [[Collections|Collection]]. Trading for resources does not affect collection progress.
You can directly sell items from Trade Menu when you have a [[Booster Cookie]] activated.
## Fairy Souls
Fairy Souls are collectibles that take the form of custom-textured heads (smaller blocks) scattered and hidden around parts of the map.
Players can interact with Fairy Souls by right-clicking them. Each soul is unique and is able to be collected only once. A message in the chat is displayed to the player each time they interact with a soul informing them they have collected the soul or previously discovered it.
Fairy Souls may be exchanged in groups of five with [[Tia the Fairy]], a fairy in the [[Wilderness]] in the south part of the Hub Island, by a sand pond. In exchange, the player will receive [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] and [[Backpack]] slots. Exchanges will slowly improve as more souls are turned in.
There are 238 fairy souls in total as of the SkyBlock v0.13 update.
- Three fairy souls are not found in the physical world but are instead obtained as a rare drop while [[Fishing]].
- Eight fairy souls can be found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] scattered in different rooms in dungeons. Specific coordinates for each fairy soul location won't be available due to randomized generated dungeon rooms with different coordinates. A few ways to discover them are by watching a walkthrough, observing images, or knowing dungeon room details, followed by memorizing each room the fairy souls in. Players can also check the [[Quests|Quest Log]] in Fairy Souls while in dungeons with the item displaying a paper named Dungeon. It'll show how many fairy souls are available in this specific dungeon. If it is missing, there are no fairy souls in that dungeon. Always check before scavenging for them.
- Note that finding them all can take a few hours even with a guide. It is recommended to watch a walkthrough in slow-motion to stay on track.
## Dungeons
The Catacombs Entrance
Dungeons are randomly generated labyrinths. Within a dungeon, there are monsters that will constantly try to harm the player. Each mob is unique in its own way and may have a chance to drop valuable items, including various [[Armor]] pieces and [[Weapons]]. There are also numerous different rooms consisting of mini-bosses, [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|puzzles]], and even [[NPC|NPCs]] to buy items from.

Aliases: "Fragranced" Brown Mushroom Paste, Fragranced Brown Mushroom Paste

["Fragranced" Brown Mushroom "Paste"]
Type: Item

Tooltip: Give this item to a Visitor for no reward, scaring them off for 24h.; Max 2 Visitors.; RARE

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] 2x [[Feastfungus]]|Recipe: [[Feastfungus]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] → &quot; Fragranced&quot; Brown Mushroom &quot; Paste&quot|
## Usage
"Fragranced" Brown Mushroom "Paste" can be given to any [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] to scare them off and prevent them from appearing for 24h. A maximum of 2 visitors can be scared off at any given time.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Giving any Visitor a; "Fragranced" Brown; Mushroom "Paste"|[NPC] <visitor>: YUCK!! What is this??|

#### Modifying
Certain brewing ingredients can be added during the brewing process to modify the potion. Other mechanics can also extend the length of the potion.

|-|-|
|[[Redstone Dust]]|+8:00|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|+16:00|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|+16:00|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]]|+16:00; +3 levels|
|[[Glowstone Dust]]|+1 level|
|[[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]]|+2 levels|
|[[Enchanted Glowstone]]|+3 levels|
|[[Gunpowder]]|Duration ÷ 2; Splash|
|[[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Splash|
|[[Potion Affinity Talisman]]|+10% Duration|
|[[Potion Affinity Ring]]|+25% Duration|
|[[Potion Affinity Artifact]]|+50% Duration|
|[[Parrot Pet]]|+40% Duration|
|[[Alchemy]]|+𝑥 Duration|
[Special]
|[[Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison]]|+5% Damage (A Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison can be added as the starting ingredient instead of a Water Bottle to add +5% damage to the final potion.)|
