# Aspect of the Leech
Aliases: AotL, Aspect Of The Leech, Aspect of the Leech (Epic), Aspect of the Leech (Rare), Aspect of the Leech (Uncommon)
Categories: Items, Craftable items
[Aspect of the Leech]
Type: Wand ([[Wands (disambiguation)]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])-EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ASPECT_OF_THE_LEECH_1; ASPECT_OF_THE_LEECH_2; ASPECT_OF_THE_LEECH_3
Tooltip: Ability: Weird Transmission RIGHT CLICK Teleport 3 blocks ahead of you.; Mana Cost: 60; UNCOMMON WAND
The Aspect of the Leech is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]-[[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Wands|Wand]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Lil' Pad]]|Recipe: [[Lil' Pad]] x64 ×2 + [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] x8 → Aspect of the Leech|
## Usage
Wand:
Aspect of the Leech
[Effects]
[Ability: Weird Transmission RIGHT CLICK; Teleport 3 blocks ahead of you.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 60]
Wand:
Aspect of the Leech (Rare) ([[Aspect of the Leech]])
[Effects]
[Ability: Weird Transmission RIGHT CLICK; Teleport 4 blocks ahead of you.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 60]
Wand:
Aspect of the Leech (Epic) ([[Aspect of the Leech]])
[Effects]
[Ability: Weird Transmission RIGHT CLICK; Teleport 5 blocks ahead of you.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 50]
## Upgrading
The Aspect of the Leech can be upgraded using 2 [[Transmission Tuner]] to extend its teleport radius from 3 to 4 blocks.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Aspect of the Leech]] 1x Aspect of the Leech 2x [[Transmission Tuner]]|Recipe: [[Transmission Tuner]] ×2 + Aspect of the Leech → Aspect of the Leech|
It can then be further upgraded using 2 [[Super Leech Modifier]] to extend its teleport radius from 4 to 5 blocks and reduce its [[Mana]] cost from 60 to 50.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Aspect of the Leech (Rare) ([[Aspect of the Leech]]) 2x [[Super Leech Modifier]]|Recipe: [[Super Leech Modifier]] ×2 + Aspect of the Leech → Aspect of the Leech|
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Aspect of the Leech.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Aspect of the Leech can now be seen in the SkyBlock Items API ([[SkyBlock API]]).|
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Aspect of the Leech.|
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Decreased rarity of base Aspect of the Leech from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]).|
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added EPIC ([[Rarity]]) upgrade to Aspect of the Leech.|

# Bait
Aliases: Baits, Fishing Bait
Categories: Items, Pages with UIs, Pages with Missing Recipes, Fishing
[Bait]
Type: Utility of Fishing
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]]), UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]), RARE ([[Rarity]])
Source: Crafting, Fishing, or Mob Drops
Uses: Give extra advantage on fishing.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 3–80 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPOOKY_BAIT; CARROT_BAIT; FISH_BAIT; CORRUPTED_BAIT; LIGHT_BAIT; DARK_BAIT; MINNOW_BAIT; SPIKED_BAIT; GLOWY_CHUM_BAIT; WOODEN_BAIT; WORM_BAIT; HOT_BAIT; SHARK_BAIT; ICE_BAIT; BLESSED_BAIT; GOLDEN_BAIT; TREASURE_BAIT; HOTSPOT_BAIT; WHALE_BAIT; FROZEN_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +25 Fishing Speed.; COMMON BAIT
Baits are items used to provide bonuses while [[Fishing]]. They give various effects and are automatically used while fishing if they are in the inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. If the player cancels casting, Bait will be refunded. The effects of a Bait last from when the player casts their [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] until they reel it back in.
## Baits
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!

|[[Shark Bait]]|[[Shark Bait]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Increases your chance to catch Sharks during the [[Fishing Festival]] by +20%.|[[Crafting]] Recipe: [[Shark Fin]] ×2 → [[Shark Bait]] x16|

### Other Baits
These items can be used as Bait, but are not categorized as such.
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Effect|Source|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Obfuscated 1 ([[Trophy Fish]])|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Grants a chance 20% chance to fish up an Obfuscated 2 ([[Trophy Fish]]).|[[Fishing]]; Using [[Corrupted Bait]] as Bait|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Obfuscated 2 ([[Trophy Fish]])|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Grants a chance 10% chance to fish up an Obfuscated 3 ([[Trophy Fish]]).|[[Fishing]]; Using Obfuscated 1 ([[Trophy Fish]]) as Bait|
## Conserving Bait
Certain items provide a chance to not consume bait on use, stacking additively:
- [[Bait Ring]]: 5% chance to not consume Bait.
- [[Spiked Atrocity]]: 10% chance to not consume Bait (takes priority over [[Bait Ring]]).
- [[Caster]]: 5%–30% chance to not consume Bait.
- [[Attributes|Fisherman]]: 1%–10% chance to not consume Bait.
- [[Stingy Sinker]]: 10% chance to not consume Bait.
This results in a maximum 60% chance to not consume Bait.
## Trivia
- The textures of Bait items include different containers based on the item [[Rarity]].
  - [[Rarity|COMMON]] Baits resemble a wooden bucket.
  - [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Baits resemble a metal bucket.
  - [[Rarity|RARE]] Baits resemble a darker metal bucket.

The Battle Disc can be obtained from [[Gifts]].

# Bridge Egg
Categories: Items, Craftable items, Raw Chicken Collection
[Bridge Egg]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Raw Chicken II (2) ([[Raw Chicken]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 10 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 8x [[Stone]] 5x [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BRIDGE_EGG
Tooltip: Creates a path of stone blocks as it travels through the air!; COMMON
A Bridge Eggs is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item unlocked at [[Raw Chicken|Raw Chicken II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Chicken II (2) ([[Raw Chicken]])|5x [[Egg]] 8x [[Stone]]|Recipe: [[Stone]] ×8 + [[Egg]] x5 → Bridge Egg|
## Usage
Throwing a Bridge Egg creates a 3x3 plus shaped (+) trail of [[Cobblestone]] on the [[Private Island]] for 2 seconds or until it hits a block.
## Tips
- The arc of a Bridge Egg varies greatly depending on the angle, with positive pitches yielding shorter trails.
  - This may be due to the Bridge Egg colliding with its own [[Cobblestone]] trail.
## Trivia
- Blocks placed by a Bridge Egg cannot be destroyed with a [[Block Zapper]].
  - A [[Builder's Ruler]], however, does work.
- Bridge Eggs are found in Bedwars, where it shares the same name and function.
- [[Cobblestone]] blocks created from a thrown Bridge Egg grant [[Mining]] XP.
## Gallery
- 5 Bridge Eggs were thrown at different pitches. The length of the trail varies greatly on horizontal directions.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Bridge Egg.|

# Cake Soul
Aliases: Cake Souls, Captured Player Cake Soul, Random Cake Soul
Categories: Items, Pages with non-auctionable items with valid auctionable item IDs, Cake
[Cake Soul]
Type: Item
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: Eating a Century Cake ([[Century Cakes]])
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CAKE_SOUL
Type: Item
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: Right-clicking a player with a Cake Soul
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable: ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CAKE_SOUL
Tooltip: Quickly click on a player several times while holding this item to capture their Soul in it!; Capturing a soul will soulbind this item.; MYTHIC
What does it all mean?
Cake Souls are items received from eating [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]]. They are often considered a collector item as they store the name of players.
## Obtaining
### Eating Century Cakes
If the player has more than 8 hours of playtime, a [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]] will have a 0.1% chance to drop a Cake Soul of its color when eaten. Chance can be increased by [[Artifact of the Century]] line. Their color corresponds to the color of cake eaten to receive it, with the exception of cyan and light blue being swapped. If a person would eat cakes in a perfectly optimized way, it would take, on average 167 days to get a cake soul.
|Appearance|Source|First Available|
|-|-|-|
|[[Pink Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Crab-Colored Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Lime Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Pet Rock Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Rose Red]]|[[Century Cakes]] Fruit Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Dandelion Yellow]]|[[Century Cakes]] Potato-Style Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Light Blue Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Barry Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Lapis Lazuli]]|[[Century Cakes]] Sea Emperor Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Ink Sac]]|[[Century Cakes]] Century Cake of the Next Dungeon Floor|Year 100|
|[[Orange Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Latest Update Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Purple Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Streamer's Century Cake|Year 100|
|[[Cocoa Beans]]|[[Century Cakes]] Chocolate Century Cake|Year 200|
|[[Bone Meal]]|[[Century Cakes]] Cloudy Century Cake|Year 200|
|[[Cyan Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Century Cake of Hype|Year 200|
|[[Cactus Green]]|[[Century Cakes]] Undead Century Cake|Year 300|
|[[Gray Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Not-a-lie Century Cake|Year 300|
|[[Magenta Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] ekaC yrutneC tfiR|Year 400|
|[[Light Gray Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Corpse-Flavored Century Cake|Year 400|
|[[Cactus Green]]|[[Century Cakes]] Long-Expired Century Cake|Year 500|
|[[Light Blue Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Century Cake of the Seven Seas|Year 500|
|[[Pink Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Starborn Century Cake|Year 500|
|[[Magenta Dye]]|[[Century Cakes]] Hephaestus' Century Cake|Year 500|
### Winning in Raffle of Century
A Cake Soul can be gained as MINOR reward during [[Raffle of the Century]].
### Gift Bag of the Century
A Cake Soul can be chosen from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]] as one of rewards.
## Usage
The Cake Soul can be used by holding right click on a player. After a moment it will turn into a "captured" Cake Soul, displaying the name of the selected player, the date the soul was captured, and the username of the player who initiated the capture. After being used, the Cake Soul will become [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] and cannot be reused.
When obtained from a [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]], the "unused" Cake Soul stores the name of the player whose island the Century Cakes were on. Players who would like to obtain a Cake Soul with the "Cake owned by" of a specific member of a [[Co-op]] should visit the player they want before eating the Century Cakes.
## Gallery
- 7[YOUTUBE] TimeDeo's captured cake soul 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] TimeDeo's captured cake soul
- Double IGN Cake Soul with no "Cake owned by" tag.[1] Double IGN Cake Soul with no "Cake owned by" tag.
- 12[ADMIN] Jayavarmen's cake soul captured by 51[MAYOR] Technoblade on 4/20/2021. 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Jayavarmen's cake soul captured by 51[[Ranks|[MAYOR]]] Technoblade on 4/20/2021.
## Trivia
- The [[Cake Soul]] Cake Soul is one of eight naturally obtainable items to have the [[Rarity|DIVINE]] rarity after being [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]], the others being [[Divan's Drill]], [[Diamond Magmafish]], [[Radioactive Vial]], [[Complete Century Cake Bundle]], [[Scorched Books]] and [[Plasma Bucket]].
- If a player uses the /nick command (available to 6[[Ranks|MVP++]] and 7[[Ranks|YOUTUBE]] rank players) to change their username and a Cake Soul is used on them, it will display the nickname on the item, instead of the players original username.
- The Cake Soul's NBT data can be viewed with various mods such as SkyBlock Addons (/sba dev then hover over the Cake Soul and click right control to copy the NBT data) or SkyBlock Extras (/sbe dev then hover over the Cake Soul and the NBT data is viewable below the item). This is often used by collectors to check the Cake owned by of an "unused" Cake Soul before they buy them.
- If a 7[[Ranks|YOUTUBE]] ranked player uses the /nick command to take a username; a second player changes their username to the same username as that of the 7[[Ranks|YOUTUBE]] ranked player; and one of the players captures the other with a Cake Soul, that player will end up with a Cake Soul with the same Captured and Captured by names. If a player got a Cake Soul with Cake owned by [player] after the 30 day name change cooldown, they can change their username back to the one displayed on the Cake Soul, to obtain one with a triple name (see Gallery).
- When a player obtains a Cake Soul from Century Cakes on their own island, the NBT tag skyblock_cake_owner is missing, resulting in used Cake Souls with no Cake owned by [PLAYER] (see Gallery).
- The Green Cake Soul used to be obtained from the [[Century Cakes|Pet Rock Century Cake]] before the [[Century Cakes|Undead ༕ Century Cake]] was added.
- The Aqua Cake Soul and Cyan Cake Soul used to be obtained from their [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]] swapped.
## History

|Nov 4, 2020|Added Cake Soul.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Added UUIDs to Cake Souls.|
|Feb 23, 2022|Removed UUIDs from newly obtained Cake Souls.|
|Feb 24, 2022|Added UUIDs back to newly obtained Cake Souls.|
|Apr 4, 2022|Added the brown variant.|
|Apr 4, 2022|Added the white variant.|
|Apr 5, 2022|The light blue variant can now be obtained.|
|Jun 2, 2022|Captured cake souls are now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed some Cake Soul capture dates not being shown in EST.|
|Sep 2, 2023|Added Lime variant.|
|Sep 2, 2023|Added Gray variant.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Updated item descriptions of Cake Soul.|
